   tive. 2:22 itō, avaḷaip paṭutta paṭukkaiyākkuvēṉ. avaḷōṭu vipacāram
   ceyvōr avaḷ tīcceyalkaḷai viṭṭuviṭṭu maṉam māṟāviṭṭāl, avarkaḷaiyum
   koṭiya vētaṉaikku uḷḷākkuvēṉ.
   tive. 2:23 avaḷuṭaiya piḷḷaikaḷaik koṉṟoḻippēṉ. appoḻutu uḷḷaṅkaḷaiyum
   itayaṅkaḷaiyum turuvi āykiṟavar nāṉē eṉpatai ellāt tiruccapaikaḷum
   aṟintukoḷḷum. uṅkaḷuḷ ovvoruvarukkum avaravar ceyalkaḷukku ēṟpak
   kaimmāṟu aḷippēṉ.
   tive. 2:24 tiyattirāvil irukkum ēṉaiyōrē, nīṅkaḷ intap pōtaṉaiyai
   ēṟkavillai. ' cāttāṉiṉ āḻnta ñāṉam ' eṉac collappaṭuvatai nīṅkaḷ aṟiya
   virumpavillai. eṉavē nāṉ uṅkaḷukkuc colvatu: uṅkaḷmītu vēṟu
   eccumaiyaiyum nāṉ cumattamāṭṭēṉ.
   tive. 2:25 nīṅkaḷ peṟṟukkoṇṭa pōtaṉaiyil nāṉ varumvarai
   piṭippuḷḷavarkaḷāy iruṅkaḷ.
   tive. 2:26 eṉ tantaiyiṭamiruntu nāṉ atikāram peṟṟiruppatupōla, veṟṟi
   peṟuvōrukkum eṉ ceyalkaḷai iṟutivarai ceyvōrukkum,
   tive. 2:27 ' vēṟṟiṉattār mītu atikāram aḷippēṉ. avarkaḷ vēṟṟiṉattārai
   iruppukkōlāl naṭattuvārkaḷ:
   tive. 2:28 kuyavarkalattaip pōla noṟukkuvārkaḷ. ' viṭiveḷḷiyaiyum
   avarkaḷukkuk koṭuppēṉ.
   tive. 2:29 kēṭkac cevi uṭaiyōr tiruccapaikaḷukkut tūya āviyār
   kūṟuvataik kēṭkaṭṭum.
   ------------
   3-m atikāram
   tive. 3:1 cartaiyil uḷḷa tiruccapaiyiṉ vāṉatūtarukku ēḻu āvikaḷaiyum
   ēḻu viṇmīṉkaḷaiyum koṇṭiruppavar kūṟuvatu ituve: uṉ ceyalkaḷai nāṉ
   aṟivēṉ. nī peyaraḷaviltāṉ uyirōṭirukkiṟāy: uṇmaiyil iṟantuviṭṭāy.
   tive. 3:2 eṉavē viḻippāyiru. uṉṉil eñciyiruppatai uṟutippaṭuttu. atu
   iṟakkum taṟuvāyil uḷḷatu. eṉ kaṭavuḷ tirumuṉ uṉ ceyalkaḷ
   niṟaivaṟṟavaiyāy irukkak kaṇṭēṉ.
   tive. 3:3 nī kaṟṟataiyum kēṭṭataiyum niṉaivil koḷ: avaṟṟaik kaṭaippiṭi:
   maṉam māṟu: nī viḻippāyiru. illaiyēl nāṉ tiruṭaṉaip pōla varuvēṉ. nāṉ
   enta nērattil uṉṉiṭam varuvēṉ eṉpatai nī aṟiyāy.
   tive. 3:4 āyiṉum, taṅkaḷ āṭaikaḷaik kaṟaippaṭuttikkoḷḷāta cilar
   cartaiyil uḷḷaṉar. avarkaḷ veṇṇāṭai aṇintu eṉṉuṭaṉ naṭantu varuvārkaḷ.
   avarkaḷ ataṟkut takuti peṟṟavarkaḷē.
   tive. 3:5 veṟṟipeṟṟōr ivvāṟu veṇṇāṭai aṇivikkappeṟuvar. vāḻviṉ
   nūliliruntu avarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷai nīkkiviṭa māṭṭēṉ. māṟāka, eṉ tantai
   muṉṉilaiyilum avaruṭaiya vāṉatūtarkaḷ muṉṉilaiyilum avarkaḷiṉ
   peyarkaḷai aṟikkaiyiṭuvēṉ.
   tive. 3:6 kēṭkac cevi uṭaiyōr tiruccapaikaḷukkut tūya āviyār kūṟuvataik
   kēṭkaṭṭum.
   tive. 3:7 ' pilatelpiyāvil uḷḷa tiruccapaiyiṉ vāṉatūtarukku ivvāṟu
   eḻutu: ' tūyavarum uṇmaiyuḷḷavarum tāvītiṉ tiṟavukōlaik koṇṭiruppavarum
   evarum pūṭṭa muṭiyātavāṟu tiṟantu viṭupavarum evarum tiṟakka
   muṭiyātavāṟu pūṭṭiviṭupavarum ' kūṟuvatu ituvē:
   tive. 3:8 uṉ ceyalkaḷai nāṉ aṟivēṉ. itō, evarālum pūṭṭa muṭiyāta
   katavai nāṉ uṉakkumuṉ tiṟantu vaittirukkiṟēṉ. ciṟitaḷavu valimaitāṉ
   uṉṉiṭam irukkiṟatu. iruppiṉum, nī eṉ vākkaik kaṭaippiṭittāy: eṉ peyarai
   maṟutalikkavillai.
   tive. 3:9 cāttāṉiṉ kūṭṭattaic cērntavarkaḷ, tāṅkaḷ yūtarkaḷ eṉak kūṟik
   koḷkiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ yūtarkaḷē alla, poyyarkaḷ. avarkaḷ uṉṉiṭam vantu,
   uṉ kālaṭiyil viḻuntu paṇiyac ceyvēṉ: nāṉ uṉmītu aṉpu celuttivarukiṟēṉ
   eṉpataiyum aṟiyacceyvēṉ.
   tive. 3:10 maṉavuṟuti tarum eṉ vākkai nī kaṭaippiṭittatāl, maṇṇulakil
   vāḻvōraic cōtikka ulaku aṉaittiṉmītum varavirukkum cōtaṉaik kālattil
   nāṉ uṉṉaik kāppāṟṟuvēṉ.
   tive. 3:11 itō, viraivil varukiṟēṉ. uṉakkuriya maṇimuṭiyai vēṟu yārum
   paṟittukkoḷḷātapaṭi pārttuk koḷ. nī peṟṟukkoṇṭatai uṟutiyākap
   paṟṟikkoḷ.
   tive. 3:12 veṟṟi peṟuvōrai eṉ kaṭavuḷiṉ kōvilil tūṇāka nāṭṭuvēṉ.
   avarkaḷ atai viṭṭu orupoḻutum nīṅkamāṭṭārkaḷ. eṉ kaṭavuḷiṉ peyaraiyum
   eṉ kaṭavuḷuṭaiya nakariṉ peyaraiyum, atāvatu eṉ kaṭavuḷiṭamiruntu
   viṇṇakattai viṭṭu iṟaṅkivarukiṉṟa putiya peyaraiyum avarkaḷmītu
   poṟippēṉ.
   tive. 3:13 kēṭkac cevi uṭaiyōr tiruccapaikaḷukkut tūya āviyār
   kūṟuvataik kēṭkaṭṭum.
   tive. 3:14 ' ilavōtikkēyāvil uḷḷa tiruccapaiyiṉ vāṉatūtarukku ivvāṟu
   eḻutu: ' āmeṉ eṉappaṭupavarum nampikkaikkuriya, uṇmaiyāṉa cāṭciyum
   kaṭavuḷatu paṭaippiṉ toṭakkamum āṉavar kūṟuvatu ituve:
   tive. 3:15 uṉ ceyalkaḷai nāṉ aṟivēṉ. nī kuḷircciyākavum illai,
   cūṭākavum illai. kuḷircciyākavō cūṭākavō iruntiruntāl ettuṇai nalamāy
   iruntirukkum.
   tive. 3:16 ivvāṟu nī kuḷircciyākavō cūṭākavō illāmal vetuvetuppāy
   iruppatāl eṉ vāyiliruntu uṉṉaik kakkiviṭuvēṉ.
   tive. 3:17 ' eṉakkuc celvam uṇṭu, vaḷamai uṇṭu, oru kuṟaiyum illai '
   eṉa nī collikkoḷḷukiṟāy. āṉāl, nī iḻinta, iraṅkattakka, vaṟiya,
   pārvaiyaṟṟa, āṭaiyaṟṟa nilaiyil irukkiṟāy eṉpatu uṉakkut teriyavillai.
   tive. 3:18 ākavē, nī celvam peṟumporuṭṭu puṭampōṭṭa poṉṉaiyum,
   āṭaiyiṉṟi veṭki niṟkum uṉ nilaiyaip piṟar kāṇātapaṭi aṇintukoḷḷa
   veṇṇāṭaiyaiyum, nī pārvai peṟumporuṭṭu uṉ kaṇkaḷil taṭavikkoḷḷa
   maruntaiyum eṉṉiṭamiruntu vilaikku vāṅkik koḷḷumāṟu uṉakku aṟivurai
   vaḻaṅkukiṟēṉ.
   tive. 3:19 nāṉ yār mītu aṉpu celuttukiṟēṉō avarkaḷaik kaṭintu taṇṭittut
   tiruttukiṟēṉ. ākavē nī āvam koṇṭu maṉam māṟu.
   tive. 3:20 itō, nāṉ katavu arukil niṉṟu taṭṭikkoṇṭirukkiṟēṉ. yārāvatu
   eṉatu kuralaik kēṭṭuk katavait tiṟantāl, nāṉ uḷḷē ceṉṟu avarkaḷōṭu
   uṇavu aruntuvēṉ: avarkaḷum eṉṉōṭu uṇavu aruntuvārkaḷ.
   tive. 3:21 nāṉ veṟṟi peṟṟu eṉ tantaiyiṉ iyaṇaiyil avarōṭu vīṟṟiruppatu
   pōla, veṟṟi peṟum evarukkumē eṉatu iyaṇaiyil eṉṉōṭu vīṟṟirukkum imai
   aḷippēṉ.
   tive. 3:22 kēṭkac cevi uṭaiyōr, tiruccapaikaḷukkut tūya āviyār
   kūṟuvataik kēṭkaṭṭum.
   ------------
   4-m atikāram
   tive. 4:1 itaṉpiṉ nāṉ oru kāṭci kaṇṭēṉ: viṇṇakattil oru katavu
   tiṟantiruntatu. nāṉ mutalil kēṭṭa atē kural ekkāḷampōla muḻaṅkiyatu: '
   ivviṭattukku ēṟivā. iṉi naṭakkavēṇṭiyatai uṉakkuk kāṭṭuvēṉ ' eṉṟatu.
   tive. 4:2 uṭaṉē tūya āvi eṉṉai āṭkoṇṭatu. viṇṇakattil ariyaṇai oṉṟu
   iruntatu. atil oruvar vīṟṟiruntār.
   tive. 4:3 avaratu tōṟṟam paṭikakkalpōlum māṇikkampōlum iruntatu.
   marakatampōṉṟa vāṉavil anta ariyaṇaiyaic cūḻntiruntatu.
   tive. 4:4 ariyaṇaiyaic cuṟṟi irupattu nāṉku ariyaṇaikaḷ
   pōṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avaṟṟil irupattu nāṉku mūpparkaḷ vīṟṟiruntārkaḷ.
   avarkaḷ veṇṇāṭai aṇintiruntārkaḷ: talaiyil poṉmuṭi cūṭiyiruntārkaḷ.
   tive. 4:5 ariyaṇaiyiliruntu miṉṉalum pēriraiccalum iṭimuḻakkamum
   kiḷampiṉa. ariyaṇaimuṉ ēḻu tīvaṭṭikaḷ erintukoṇṭiruntaṉa. avai
   kaṭavuḷiṉ ēḻu āvikaḷē.
   tive. 4:6 ariyaṇai muṉ paḷiṅkaiyotta tauinta kaṭal pōṉṟa oṉṟu
   teṉpaṭṭatu. naṭuvil ariyaṇaiyaic cuṟṟilum nāṉku uyirkaḷ kāṇpaṭṭaṉa.
   muṉpuṟamum piṉpuṟamum avaṟṟukkuk kaṇkaḷ iruntaṉa.
   tive. 4:7 avvuyirkaḷuḷ mutalāvatu ciṅkampōlum, iraṇṭāvatu iḷaṅkāḷai
   pōlum tōṉṟiṉa. mūṉṟāvataṟku maṉita mukam iruntatu, nāṉkāvatu paṟakkum
   kaḻukai ottiruntatu.
   tive. 4:8 inta nāṉku uyirkaḷ ovvoṉṟukkum āṟu ciṟakukaḷ iruntaṉa: uḷḷum
   puṟamum kaṇkaḷ niṟaintiruntaṉa. ' tūyavar, tūyavar, tūyavar, ellām
   valla kaṭavuḷākiya āṇṭavar: iruntavarum irukkiṉṟavarum
   varavirukkiṉṟavarum ivarē ' eṉṟu anta uyirkaḷ allum pakalum iṭaiyaṟātu
   pāṭikkoṇṭiruntaṉa.
   tive. 4:9 ariyaṇaiyil vīṟṟiruppavarai, eṉṟeṉṟum vāḻpavarai avai pōṟṟip
   pukaḻntu avarukku naṉṟi celuttiyapōtellām,
   tive. 4:10 irupattu nāṉku mūpparkaḷ ariyaṇaiyil vīṟṟiruntavar muṉ
   viḻuntu, eṉṟeṉṟum vāḻkiṉṟa avarai vaṇaṅkiṉārkaḷ. taṅkaḷ poṉmuṭikaḷai
   ariyaṇaimuṉ vaittu,
   tive. 4:11 ' eṅkaḷ āṇṭavarē, eṅkaḷ kaṭavuḷē, māṭciyum māṇpum
   vallamaiyum peṟa nīr takuti peṟṟavar: ēṉeṉil aṉaittaiyum paṭaittavar
   nīrē. umatu tiruvuḷappaṭiyē avai uṇṭāyiṉa, paṭaikkappaṭṭaṉa ' eṉṟu
   pāṭiṉārkaḷ.
   ---------------
   5-m atikāram
   tive. 5:1 ariyaṇaiyil vīṟṟiruntavaratu valakkaiyil oru curuḷēṭṭaik
   kaṇṭēṉ. atil uḷḷum puṟamum eḻutiyiruntatu: atu ēḻu muttirai poṟikkap
   peṟṟu mūṭappaṭṭiruntatu.
   tive. 5:2 ' muttiraikaḷai uṭaittu, ēṭṭaip pirikkat takuti peṟṟavar yār?
   ' eṉṟu valimaimikka vāṉatūtar oruvar uratta kuralil muḻaṅkak kaṇṭēṉ.
   tive. 5:3 nūlait tiṟantu paṭikka viṇṇulakilō maṇṇulakilō kīḻulakilō
   irunta evarālum iyalavillai.
   tive. 5:4 curuḷēṭṭaip pirittup paṭikkat takuti peṟṟavar evaraiyum
   kāṇavillaiyē eṉṟu nāṉ tēmpi aḻutēṉ.
   tive. 5:5 appoḻutu mūpparuḷ oruvar eṉṉiṭam, ' aḻātē, yūtā kulattiṉ
   ciṅkamum tāvītiṉ kulakkoḻuntumāṉavar veṟṟi peṟṟu viṭṭār: avar anta ēḻu
   muttiraikaḷaiyum uṭaittu ēṭṭaip pirittu viṭuvār ' eṉṟu kūṟiṉār.
   tive. 5:6 anta nāṉku uyirkaḷum mūpparkaḷum puṭai cūḻa, ariyaṇai naṭuvil
   āṭṭukkuṭṭi oṉṟu niṟkak kaṇṭēṉ. kollappaṭṭatupōl atu kāṇappaṭṭatu.
   ataṟku ēḻu kompukaḷum ēḻu kaṇkaḷum iruntaṉa. akkaṇkaḷ maṇṇulakeṅkum
   aṉuppappeṟṟa kaṭavuḷiṉ ēḻu āvikaḷē.
   tive. 5:7 āṭṭukkuṭṭi muṉceṉṟu, ariyaṇaiyil vīṟṟiruntaviṉ
   valakkaiyiliruntu anta ēṭṭai eṭuttatu.
   tive. 5:8 appoḻutu anta nāṉku uyirkaḷum irupattu nāṉku mūpparkaḷum
   āṭṭukkuṭṭimuṉ vīḻntārkaḷ: varkaḷ ovvoruvarum yāḻum, cāmpirāṇi niṟainta
   poṟ kiṇṇaṅkaḷum vaittiruntārkaḷ. iṟaimakkaḷiṉ vēṇṭutalkaḷē akkiṇṇaṅkaḷ.
   tive. 5:9 avarkaḷ putiyatoru pāṭalaip pāṭikkoṇṭiruntārkaḷ: ' ēṭṭai
   eṭukkavum ataṉ muttiraikaḷai uṭaittup pirikkavum takuti peṟṟavar nīrē.
   nīr kollappaṭṭīr: umatu irattattāl kulam, moḻi, nāṭu, makkaḷiṉam ākiya
   aṉaittiṉiṉṟum makkaḷaik kaṭavuḷukkeṉṟu vilai koṭuttu mīṭṭukkoṇṭīr.
   tive. 5:10 āṭciyurimai peṟṟavarkaḷākavum kurukkaḷākavum avarkaḷai eṅkaḷ
   kaṭavuḷukkāka ēṟpaṭuttiṉīr. avarkaḷ maṇṇulakiṉmītu āṭci celuttuvārkaḷ.
   '
   tive. 5:11 toṭarntu nāṉ pārttuk koṇṭirukkumpōtu, ariyaṇaiyaiyum
   uyirkaḷaiyum mūpparkaḷaiyum cuṟṟi niṉṟa kōṭik kaṇakkāṉa vāṉatūtarkaḷiṉ
   kuralaik kēṭṭēṉ:
   tive. 5:12 ' kollappaṭṭa āṭṭukkuṭṭi vallamaiyum celvamum ñāṉamum
   āṟṟalum māṇpum perumaiyum pukaḻcciyum peṟat takuti peṟṟatu ' eṉṟu
   avarkaḷ uratta kuralil pāṭik koṇṭiruntārkaḷ.
   tive. 5:13 piṉpu, viṇṇulaku, maṇṇulaku, kīḻulaku, kaṭal eṅkum irunta
   paṭaippukaḷ aṉaittum, avaṟṟil irunta ovvoṉṟum, ' ariyaṇaiyil
   vīṟṟiruppavarukkum āṭṭukkuṭṭikkum pukaḻcciyum māṇpum perumaiyum āṟṟalum
   eṉṟeṉṟum uriyaṉa ' eṉṟu pāṭak kēṭṭēṉ.
   tive. 5:14 ataṟku anta nāṉku uyirkaḷum, ' āmeṉ ' eṉṟaṉa. mūpparkaḷ
   viḻuntu vaṇaṅkiṉārkaḷ.
   ------------
   6-m atikāram
   tive. 6:1 piṉṉar āṭṭukkuṭṭi ēḻu muttiraikaḷuḷ mutalāvatai uṭaikkak
   kaṇṭēṉ. appoḻutu nāṉku uyirkaḷuḷ mutalāvatu ' vā ' eṉṟu muṭimuḻakkam
   pōṉṟa kuralil aḻaikkak kēṭṭēṉ.
   tive. 6:2 uṭaṉē, oru veḷḷaik kutiraiyaik kaṇṭēṉ. ataṉmēl
   ēṟiyiruntavariṭam oru vil iruntatu. avarukku vākai cūṭṭappaṭṭatu.
   veṟṟimēl veṟṟi koḷḷa avar vauiyē puṟappaṭṭuc ceṉṟār.
   tive. 6:3 āṭṭukkuṭṭi iraṇṭāvatu muttiraiyai uṭaittapoḻutu avvuyirkaḷuḷ
   iraṇṭāvatu, ' vā ' eṉṟu aḻaikkak kēṭṭēṉ.
   tive. 6:4 appoḻutu civappuk kutirai oṉṟu vauiyē vantatu. ataṉmēl
   ēṟiyiruntavarukku, ulakil amaitiyaik kulaikka atikāram koṭukkappaṭṭatu:
   periyatoru vāḷum aḷikkappaṭṭatu. maṉitar tammuḷ oruvarai oruvar
   paṭukolai ceyya vēṇṭumeṉṟē ivai avarukkuk koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa.
   tive. 6:5 āṭṭukkuṭṭi mūṉṟāvatu muttiraiyai uṭaittapoḻutu, avvuyirkaḷuḷ
   mūṉṟāvatu ' vā ' eṉṟu aḻaikkak kēṭṭēṉ: toṭarntu oru kaṟuppuk
   kutiraiyaik kaṇṭēṉ. ataṉmēl ēṟiyiruntavaruṭaiya kaiyil oru niṟaikōl
   iruntatu.
   tive. 6:6 nāṉku uyirkaḷiṉ naṭuvil maṉitak kural pōṉṟa ōcai oṉṟu
   kēṭṭatu. atu, ' oru teṉiyattukku kōtumai araippaṭi: vāṟkōtumai oṉṟaraip
   paṭi: eṇṇeyaiyum tirāṭcai iracattaiyum cētappaṭuttavēṇṭām ' eṉṟatu.
   tive. 6:7 āṭṭukkuṭṭi nāṉkāvatu muttiraiyai uṭaittapoḻutu avvuyirkaḷuḷ
   nāṉkāvatu, ' vā ' eṉṟu aḻaikkak kēṭṭēṉ.
   tive. 6:8 toṭarntu vauiṟiya paccaik kutirai oṉṟaik kaṇṭēṉ. ataṉmēl
   ēṟiyiruntaviṉ peyar cāvu. pātāḷamum avarōṭu ceṉṟatu. vāḷ, pañcam,
   koḷḷainōy ākiyavaṟṟālum maṇṇulakiṉ vilaṅkukaḷālum ulakiṉ kāl pakutiyai
   aḻikka cāvukkum pātāḷattukkum atikāram aḷikkappaṭṭatu.
   tive. 6:9 āṭṭukkuṭṭi aintāvatu muttiraiyai uṭaittapoḻutu, kaṭavuḷiṉ
   vākkai aṟivittuc cāṉṟu pakarntataṟkākak kolai ceyyappaṭṭavarkaḷiṉ
   āṉmākkaḷaip palipīṭattiṉ aṭiyil kaṇṭēṉ.
   tive. 6:10 avarkaḷ uratta kuralil, ' tūymaiyum uṇmaiyum uḷḷa talaivarē,
   evvaḷavu kālam ulakil vāḻvōrukku nīr tīrppu aḷikkāmal iruppīr? eṅkaḷaik
   kolai ceytataṉ poruṭṭu evvaḷavu kālam avarkaḷaip paḻivāṅkāmal iruppīr?
   ' eṉṟu kēṭṭārkaḷ.
   tive. 6:11 avarkaḷ ovvoruvarukkum oru veṇmaiyāṉa toṅkalāṭai
   aḷikkappaṭṭatu. iṉṉum ciṟitu nēram, atāvatu avarkaḷiṉ uṭaṉ
   paṇiyāḷarkaḷāṉa cakōtarar cakōtarikaḷum avarkaḷaippōlavē
   kollappaṭavirunta kālam niṟaivēṟum varai avarkaḷ poṟuttirukkumāṟu
   avarkaḷukkuc collappaṭṭatu.
   tive. 6:12 āṭṭukkuṭṭi āṟāvatu muttiraiyai uṭaikkak kaṇṭēṉ. appoḻutu
   periyatoru nilanaṭukkam ēṟpaṭṭatu. katiravaṉ kaṟuppuc cākkut tuṇi pōlak
   kaṟuttatu. nilavu muḻuvatum irattam pōl civantatu.
   tive. 6:13 perum kāṟṟu aṭikkumpoḻutu attimarattiliruntu kāykaḷ etirvatu
   pōṉṟu viṇmīṉkaḷ nilattiṉmītu viḻuntaṉa.
   tive. 6:14 curuḷēṭu curuṭṭappaṭuvatupōla vāṉamum curuṭṭappaṭṭu
   maṟaintatu. malaikaḷ, tīvukaḷ ellām nilai peyarntupōyiṉa.
   tive. 6:15 maṇṇulakil aracarkaḷ, uyarkuṭi makkaḷ, āyirattavar
   talaivarkaḷ, celvar, valiyōr, aṭimaikaḷ, urimaik kuṭimakkaḷ ākiya
   aṉaivarum kukaikaḷilum malaip pāṟaikaḷiṉ iṭukkukaḷilum oḷintu
   koṇṭārkaḷ.
   tive. 6:16 avarkaḷ anta malaikaḷiṭamum pāṟaikaḷiṭamum, ' eṅkaḷmītu
   viḻuṅkaḷ, ariyaṇai mēl vīṟṟiruppavaruṭaiya mukattiṉiṉṟum āṭṭukkuṭṭiyiṉ
   ciṉattiṉiṉṟum eṅkaḷai maṟaittukkoḷḷuṅkaḷ:
   tive. 6:17 ēṉeṉil, avarkaḷatu ciṉam vauippaṭum koṭiya nāḷ vantuviṭṭatu.
   ataṟkumuṉ niṟka yārāl iyalum? ' eṉṟu pulampiṉārkaḷ.
   -----------------
   7-m atikāram
   tive 7:1 itaṉpiṉ ulakiṉ nāṉku mūlaikaḷilum nāṉku vāṉatūtarkaḷ niṟkak
   kaṇṭēṉ. ulakiṉ mītum kaṭal mītum marattiṉmītum kāṟṟu vīcātavāṟu kāṟṟu
   vakaikaḷ nāṉkiṉaiyum avarkaḷ kaṭṭuppaṭutti vaittiruntārkaḷ.
   tive 7:2 katiravaṉ eḻum ticaiyiliruntu maṟṟoru vāṉatūtar eḻuntu varak
   kaṇṭēṉ. vāḻum kaṭavuḷiṉ muttirai avariṭam iruntatu. nilattaiyum
   kaṭalaiyum aḻikka atikāram peṟṟirunta anta nāṉku vāṉatūtarkaḷaiyum avar
   uratta kuralil aḻaittu
   tive 7:3 "eṅkaḷ kaṭavuḷuṭaiya paṇiyāḷarkaḷiṉ neṟṟiyil nāṅkaḷ
   muttiraiyiṭumvarai nilattaiyō kaṭalaiyō marattaiyō aḻikka vēṇṭām" eṉṟu
   avarkaḷiṭam kūṟiṉār.
   tive 7:4 muttiraiyiṭappaṭṭavarkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkaipaṟṟic collak kēṭṭēṉ.
   isrēyal makkaḷiṉ kulaṅkaḷ aṉaittilum muttiraiyiṭappaṭṭavarkaḷ ōr
   ilaṭcattu nāṟpattunāṉku āyiram.
   tive 7:5 yūtā kulattil muttiraiyiṭappaṭṭavarkaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭu āyiram,
   rūpaṉ kulattil paṉṉiraṇṭu āyiram, kāttu kulattil paṉṉiraṇṭu āyiram,
   tive 7:6 ācēr kulattil paṉṉiraṇṭu āyiram, naptali kulattil paṉṉiraṇṭu
   āyiram, maṉācē kulattil paṉṉiraṇṭu āyiram,
   tive 7:7 cimiyōṉ kulattil paṉṉiraṇṭu āyiram, lēvi kulattil paṉṉiraṇṭu
   āyiram, icakkār kulattil paṉṉiraṇṭu āyiram,
   tive 7:8 cepulōṉ kulattil paṉṉiraṇṭu āyiram, yōcēppu kulattil
   paṉṉiraṇṭu āyiram, peṉyamiṉ kulattil paṉṉiraṇṭu āyiram.
   tive 7:9 itaṉpiṉ yārālum eṇṇikkaiyiṭa muṭiyāta perum tiraḷāṉa makkaḷaik
   kaṇṭēṉ. avarkaḷ ellā nāṭṭaiyum, kulattaiyum makkaḷiṉattaiyum moḻiyaiyum
   cārntavarkaḷ. ariyaṇaikkum āṭṭukkuṭṭikkum muṉpāka niṉṟukoṇṭiruntārkaḷ.
   veṇmaiyāṉa toṅkalāṭai aṇintavarkaḷāyk kaiyil kuruttōlaikaḷaip
   piṭittiruntārkaḷ.
   tive 7:10 avarkaḷ, "ariyaṇaiyil vīṟṟirukkum eṅkaḷ kaṭavuḷiṭamiruntum
   āṭṭukkuṭṭiyiṭamiruntumē mīṭpu varukiṟatu" eṉṟu uratta kuralil
   pāṭiṉārkaḷ.
   tive 7:11 appoḻutu vāṉatūtarkaḷ aṉaivarum ariyaṇaiyaiyum mūpparkaḷaiyum
   nāṉku uyirkaḷaiyum cūḻntu niṉṟukoṇṭiruntārkaḷ. piṉ ariyaṇaimuṉ mukam
   kuppuṟa viḻuntu kaṭavuḷai vaṇaṅkiṉārkaḷ.
   tive 7:12 "āmeṉ, pukaḻcciyum perumaiyum ñāṉamum naṉṟiyum māṇpum
   vallamaiyum valimaiyum eṅkaḷ kaṭavuḷukkē eṉṟeṉṟum uriyaṉa. āmeṉ" eṉṟu
   pāṭiṉārkaḷ.
   tive 7:13 mūpparkaḷuḷ oruvar, "veṇmaiyāṉa toṅkalāṭai aṇintuḷḷa ivarkaḷ
   yār? eṅkiruntu vantavarkaḷ teriyumā?" eṉṟu eṉṉai viṉaviṉār.
   tive 7:14 nāṉ avariṭam "eṉ talaivarē, atu umakkuttāṉ teriyum" eṉṟēṉ.
   ataṟku avar eṉṉiṭam kūṟiyatu: "ivarkaḷ koṭiya vētaṉaiyiliruntu
   mīṇṭavarkaḷ. taṅkaḷiṉ toṅkalāṭaikaḷai āṭṭukkuṭṭiyiṉ irattattil tuvaittu
   veṇmaiyākkik koṇṭavarkaḷ.
   tive 7:15 itaṉāl kaṭavuḷatu ariyaṇai muṉ niṉṟukoṇṭu avaratu kōvilil
   allum pakalum avarai vaḻipaṭṭuvarukiṟārkaḷ. ariyaṇaiyil vīṟṟiruppavar
   avarkaḷiṭaiyē kuṭikoṇṭu avarkaḷaip pātukāppār.
   tive 7:16 iṉi avarkaḷukkup paciyō tākamō irā. katiravaṉō evvakai
   veppamō avarikaḷait tākkā.
   tive 7:17 ēṉeṉil ariyaṇai naṭuvil irukkum āṭṭukkuṭṭi avarkaḷai mēykkum
   vāḻvu aḷikkum nīruṟṟukaḷukku vaḻinaṭattic cellum. kaṭavuḷ avarkaḷiṉ
   kaṇṇīr aṉaittaiyum tuṭaittuviṭuvār."
   --------
   8-m atikāram
   tive. 8:1 āṭṭukkuṭṭi ēḻāvatu muttiraiyai uṭaittapoḻutu viṇṇakattil
   ēṟattāḻa araimaṇi nēram amaiti nilaviyatu.
   tive. 8:2 piṉ kaṭavuḷmuṉ niṉṟu koṇṭirunta ēḻu vāṉatūtarkaḷaik kaṇṭēṉ.
   avarkaḷukku ēḻu ekkāḷaṅkaḷ koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa.
   tive. 8:3 maṟṟoru vāṉatūtar poṉ tūpak kiṇṇam ēntiyavarāyp palipīṭattiṉ
   arukil vantu niṉṟār. ariyaṇaimuṉ irunta poṉ palipīṭattiṉmītu iṟaimakkaḷ
   aṉaivarum ceyta vēṇṭutalkaḷōṭu paṭaikkumāṟu avarukku mikutiyāṉa
   cāmpirāṇi vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu.
   tive. 8:4 accāmpirāṇip pukai iṟaimakkaḷiṉ vēṇṭutalkaḷōṭu cērntu
   vāṉatūtariṉ kaiyiliruntu kaṭavuḷ tirumuṉ eḻumpic ceṉṟatu.
   tive. 8:5 piṟaku anta vāṉatūtar tūpak kiṇṇattai eṭuttu, palipīṭattil
   irunta neruppiṉāl atai nirappi, maṇṇulakiṉmītu vīciyeṟintār. uṭaṉē
   iṭimuḻakkamum pēriraiccalum miṉṉalum nilanaṭukkamum uṇṭāyiṉa.
   tive. 8:6 appoḻutu ēḻu ekkāḷaṅkaḷaik koṇṭirunta ēḻu vāṉatūtarkaḷum
   avaṟṟai muḻakka āyattamāṉārkaḷ.
   tive. 8:7 mutal vāṉatūtar ekkāḷam muḻakkiṉār. uṭaṉē irattattōṭu kalanta
   kalmaḻaiyum neruppum nilattiṉmītu vantu viḻuntaṉa. nilattiṉ mūṉṟil oru
   pakuti aḻintu pōṉatu: maraṅkaḷuḷ mūṉṟil oru pakutiyum tīkkiraiyāṉatu:
   pacumpul ellāmē cuṭṭerikkappaṭṭatu.
   tive. 8:8 iraṇṭām vāṉatūtar ekkāḷam muḻakkiṉār. uṭaṉē tīppaṟṟiyinta
   periya malaipōṉṟa oṉṟu kaṭalukkuḷ eṟiyappaṭṭatu. itaṉāl kaṭaliṉ mūṉṟil
   oru pakuti irattamāka māṟiyatu.
   tive. 8:9 kaṭalvāḻ uyiṉaṅkaḷuḷ mūṉṟil oru pakuti maṭintatu: kappalkaḷuḷ
   mūṉṟil oru pakuti aḻintatu.
   tive. 8:10 mūṉṟām vāṉatūtar ekkāḷam muḻakkiṉār. uṭaṉē tīvaṭṭipōṉṟu
   erintukoṇṭirunta periya viṇmīṉ oṉṟu vāṉiliruntu pāyntu vantu āṟukaḷuḷ
   mūṉṟil orupakutiyilum nīrūṟṟukaḷilum viḻuntatu.
   tive. 8:11 anta viṇmīṉukku ' eṭṭi ' eṉpatu peyar. ākavē taṇṇil mūṉṟil
   oru pakuti eṭṭipōlak kacappāṉatu. ivvāṟu antak kacappāṉa nīraik kuṭitta
   maṉitar palar iṟantaṉar.
   tive. 8:12 nāṉkām vāṉatūtar ekkāḷam muḻakkiṉār. uṭaṉē katiravaṉiṉ
   mūṉṟil oru pakutiyum nilāviṉ mūṉṟil oru pakutiyum viṇmīṉkaḷuḷ mūṉṟil
   oru pakutiyum tākkuṇṭaṉa. itaṉāl avaṟṟuḷ mūṉṟil oru pakuti
   iruḷaṭaintatu: pakaliṉ mūṉṟil oru pakuti oḷi kuṉṟiyatu. iravukkum
   avvāṟē āyiṟṟu.
   tive. 8:13 itaṉpiṉ nāṉ oru kāṭci kaṇṭēṉ. naṭuvāṉil paṟantu koṇṭirunta
   kaḻuku oṉṟu uratta kuralil, ' maṟṟa mūṉṟu vāṉatūtarkaḷum ekkāḷaṅkaḷai
   itō muḻakkavirukkiṟārkaḷ. antō. ulakil vāḻvōrkku kēṭu varum. aiyakō. '
   eṉṟu kattak kēṭṭēṉ.
   -------------
   9-m atikāram
   tive. 9:1 piṟaku aintām vāṉatūtar ekkāḷam muḻakkiṉār. appoḻutu
   vāṉiliruntu nilattiṉmītu viḻuntu kiṭanta oru viṇmīṉaik kaṇṭēṉ. paṭukuḻi
   vāyiliṉ tiṟavukōl ataṟkuk koṭukkappaṭṭatu.
   tive. 9:2 atu paṭukuḻi vāyilait tiṟakkavē, perum cūḷaiyiliruntu pukai
   eḻuvatu pōl akkuḻiyiliruntu pukai kiḷampiyatu. appukaiyāl katiravaṉum
   vāṉvauiyum iruṇṭu pōyiṉa.
   tive. 9:3 pukaiyiliruntu veṭṭukkiḷikaḷ kiḷampi nilattukku vantaṉa.
   nilattil ūrntu tiriyum tēḷkaḷukku uḷḷa āṟṟal avaṟṟukkuk
   koṭukkappaṭṭatu.
   tive. 9:4 nilattiṉ puṟpūṇṭukaḷukkō maraṅkaḷukkō tīṅku iḻaikkāmal,
   taṅkaḷ neṟṟiyil kaṭavuḷiṉ muttirai illātavarkaḷukku maṭṭum tīṅku
   ceyyumāṟu avaṟṟukkuk kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭappaṭṭatu.
   tive. 9:5 āṉāl avarkaḷaik kollāmal aintu mātam varai vataikka maṭṭum
   veṭṭukkiḷikaḷukkuk kaṭṭaḷai koṭukkappaṭṭatu. tēḷ maṉitaraik koṭṭit
   tuṉpuṟuttuvatupōl avai avarkaḷai vataittaṉa.
   tive. 9:6 akkālattil maṉitar cāvait tēṭuvārkaḷ: āṉāl cāka māṭṭārkaḷ.
   cāka virumpuvārkaḷ: āṉāl cāvu avarkaḷai aṇukātu.
   tive. 9:7 pōrukku āyattamāy irukkum kutiraikaḷaippōl anta veṭṭukkiḷikaḷ
   kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ talaikaḷil poṉ muṭikaḷ pōṉṟavai teṉpaṭṭaṉa.
   avaṟṟiṉ mukaṅkaḷ maṉita mukaṅkaḷpōl iruntaṉa.
   tive. 9:8 avaṟṟiṉ muṭi peṇkaḷiṉ kūntalaiyum, paṟkaḷ ciṅkaṅkaḷiṉ
   paṟkaḷaiyum ottiruntaṉa.
   tive. 9:9 avaṟṟiṉ mārpil irumpālāṉa mārpuk kavacam aṇintiruntatu pōlat
   tōṉṟiyatu. ciṟakukaḷiṉ iraiccal pōrukku viraiyum tērppaṭaiyiṉ iraiccal
   pōṉṟu iruntatu.
   tive. 9:10 tēḷkaḷaip pōl avai vālum, koṭukkum koṇṭiruntaṉa. aintu mātam
   maṉitarukkut tīṅku iḻaikkum āṟṟal avaṟṟiṉ vālkaḷil iruntatu.
   tive. 9:11 paṭukuḻiyiṉ vāṉatūtarē avaṟṟukku aracaṉ. avarukku epirēya
   moḻiyil ' apattōṉ ' eṉṟum, kirēkka moḻiyil ' appolliyōṉ ' eṉṟum peyar.
   tive. 9:12 mutalāvatu kēṭu kaṭantuviṭṭatu. itō. iṉṉum iraṇṭu kēṭukaḷ
   varavirukkiṉṟaṉa.
   tive. 9:13 piṟaku āṟām vāṉatūtar ekkāḷam muḻakkiṉār. appoḻutu kaṭavuḷ
   tirumuṉ irunta poṉ palipīṭattiṉ nāṉku kompukaḷiliruntum eḻunta oru
   kuralaik kēṭṭēṉ.
   tive. 9:14 akkural anta vāṉatūtariṭam, ' yūppirattīcu pērāṟṟiṉ arukil
   kaṭṭappaṭṭuk kiṭakkum vāṉatūtar nālvaraiyum aviḻttuviṭu ' eṉṟatu.
   tive. 9:15 avvāṟē maṉitaruḷ mūṉṟil oru pakutiyiṉaraik kollumpaṭi
   kuṟikkappaṭṭirunta āṇṭu, mātam, nāḷ, maṇikkāka āyattamāy irunta anta
   nāṉku vāṉatūtarkaḷum aviḻttuviṭappaṭṭārkaḷ.
   tive. 9:16 kutiraippaṭaiyiṉ eṇṇikkaiyaic collak kēṭṭēṉ. atu irupatu
   kōṭi.
   tive. 9:17 nāṉ kaṇṭa kāṭciyil, kutiraikaḷaiyum avaṟṟiṉ mēl
   ēṟiyiruntavarkaḷaiyum kaṇṭēṉ. avarkaḷ neruppu, patumarākam, kantakam
   ākiyavaṟṟiṉ niṟaṅkaḷaik koṇṭa mārpuk kavacaṅkaḷai aṇintiruntārkaḷ.
   akkutiraikaḷiṉ talaikaḷ ciṅkaṅkaḷiṉ talaikaḷ pōṉṟu iruntaṉa: avaṟṟiṉ
   vāyiliruntu neruppum pukaiyum kantakamum vauivantaṉa.
   tive. 9:18 avaṟṟiṉ vāyiliruntu vauippaṭṭa neruppu, pukai, kantakam
   ākiya immūṉṟu vātaikaḷāl maṉitaruḷ mūṉṟil oru pakutiyiṉar
   kollappaṭṭaṉar.
   tive. 9:19 akkutiraikaḷiṉ āṟṟal avaṟṟiṉ vāyilum vālilum iruntatu.
   avaṟṟiṉ vālkaḷ pāmpupōṉṟu iruntaṉa. avai taṅkaḷ talaiyaikkoṇṭu tīṅku
   iḻaittu vantaṉa.
   tive. 9:20 anta vātaikaḷāl kollappaṭāmal eñciya maṉitarkaḷ taṅkaḷ
   ceyalkaḷai viṭṭu maṉam māṟavillai. pēykaḷaiyum poṉ, veḷḷi, veṇkalam,
   kal, maram ākiyavaṟṟāl ceyyappaṭṭa, pārkkavō kēṭkavō naṭakkavō muṭiyāta
   cilaikaḷaiyum vaṇaṅkuvatai avarkaḷ viṭṭuviṭavillai.
   tive. 9:21 tāṅkaḷ ceytuvanta kolai, pillicūṉiyam, parattaimai, kaḷavu
   ākiyavaṟṟai viṭṭu avarkaḷ maṉam māṟavillai.
   -----------
   10-m atikāram
   tive. 10:1 piṉ valimaimikka vēṟoru vāṉatūtar viṇṇakattiliruntu
   iṟaṅkivarak kaṇṭēṉ. avar mēkattai āṭaiyāka aṇintiruntār. avaratu
   talaikkumēl oru vāṉavil iruntatu: avaratu mukam katiravaṉpōlavum kālkaḷ
   nerupput tūṇkaḷpōlavum iruntaṉa.
   tive. 10:2 tiṟakkappaṭṭa oru ciṟiya curuḷēṭṭai avar tam kaiyil
   vaittiruntār. tam valatukālaik kaṭaliṉ mītum iṭatukālai nilattiṉ mītum
   vaittiruntār.
   tive. 10:3 ciṅkam karccippatu pōl uratta kuralil kattiṉār. ivvāṟu '
   avar kattiyapoḻutu ēḻu iṭikaḷ muḻaṅki etirolittaṉa.
   tive. 10:4 anta ēḻu iṭikaḷum muḻaṅkiya poḻutu nāṉ eḻuta āyattamāṉēṉ.
   āṉāl viṇṇakattiliruntu vanta oru kural, ' ēḻu iṭikaḷum coṉṉatai
   maṟaittu vai: eḻutātē ' eṉṟu collak kēṭṭēṉ.
   tive. 10:5 nāṉ kaṭaliṉmītum nilattiṉ mītum niṟkak kaṇṭa vāṉatūtar tam
   valakkaiyai viṇṇakattai nōkki uyarttiṉār.
   tive. 10:6 viṇṇaiyum atil uḷḷavaṟṟaiyum, maṇṇaiyum atil uḷḷavaṟṟaiyum,
   kaṭalaiyum atil uḷḷavaṟṟaiyum paṭaitta eṉṟeṉṟum vāḻum kaṭavuḷiṉ peyarāl
   āṇaiyiṭṭu, ' iṉit tāmatam kūṭātu.
   tive. 10:7 ēḻām vāṉatūtar ekkāḷam muḻakkappōkum kālattil, kaṭavuḷ tam
   paṇiyāḷarkaḷāṉa iṟaivākkiṉarkaḷukku aṟivittiruntapaṭiyē avaratu
   maṟaivāṉa tiṭṭam niṟaivēṟum ' eṉṟār.
   tive. 10:8 viṇṇakattiliruntu nāṉ kēṭṭa kural eṉṉiṭam mīṇṭum pēci, '
   kaṭaliṉ mītum nilattiṉmītum niṟkiṉṟa vāṉatūtariṉ kaiyil uḷḷa
   pirikkappaṭṭa curuḷēṭṭai nī pōy vāṅkik koḷ ' eṉṟatu.
   tive. 10:9 nāṉum anta vāṉatūtariṭam ceṉṟu, antac ciṟṟēṭṭai eṉṉiṭam
   tarumpaṭi kēṭṭēṉ. avarō, ' itai eṭuttut tiṉṟuviṭu: itu uṉ vayiṟṟil
   kacakkum, āṉāl vāyil tēṉaippōl iṉikkum ' eṉṟu eṉṉiṭam coṉṉār.
   tive. 10:10 uṭaṉē vāṉatūtariṉ kaiyiliruntu antac ciṟṟēṭṭai eṭuttut
   tiṉṟēṉ. atu eṉ vāyil tēṉaip pōl iṉittatu: āṉāl atait tiṉṟapoḻutu eṉ
   vayiṟṟil kacantatu.
   tive. 10:11 ' palvēṟu makkaḷiṉattār, nāṭṭiṉar, moḻiyiṉar, maṉṉarpaṟṟi
   nī mīṇṭum iṟaivākkuraikka vēṇṭum ' eṉṟu eṉakkuc collappaṭṭatu.
   ------------
   11-m atikāram
   tive. 11:1 piṉpu kuccipōṉṟa ōr aḷavu kōl eṉṉiṭam koṭukkappaṭṭatu. '
   eḻuntu, kaṭavuḷiṉ kōvilaiyum palipīṭattaiyum aḷaviṭu: aṅku
   vaḻipaṭuvōraik kaṇakkiṭu.
   tive. 11:2 āṉāl kōvilukku vauiyē uḷḷa muṟṟattai aḷakkāmal viṭṭu viṭu:
   ēṉeṉil atu vēṟṟiṉattārukkuk koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tirunakarai avarkaḷ
   nāṟpattiraṇṭu mātam mitittuc cīraḻippārkaḷ.
   tive. 11:3 nāṉ eṉ cāṭcikaḷuḷ iruvarai aṉuppuvēṉ. avarkaḷ cākku uṭai
   uṭutti, āyirattu irunūṟṟu aṟupatu nāḷum iṟaivākkuraippārkaḷ ' eṉṟu
   eṉakkuc collappaṭṭatu.
   tive. 11:4 maṇṇulakiṉ āṇṭavar tirumuṉ niṟkum iraṇṭu olivamaraṅkaḷum
   iraṇṭu viḷakkuttaṇṭukaḷumē anta iru cāṭcikaḷ.
   tive. 11:5 yārāvatu avarkaḷukkut tīṅku iḻaikka virumpiṉāl avarkaḷatu
   vāyiliruntu tī kiḷampi antap pakaivarkaḷaic cuṭṭerittuviṭum.
   avarkaḷukkut tīṅku iḻaikka virumpuvōr ivvāṟu kollappaṭuvatu uṟuti.
   tive. 11:6 tāṅkaḷ iṟaivākkuraikkum kālattil maḻai poḻiyātavāṟu vāṉattai
   aṭaittu viṭa avarkaḷukku atikāram uṇṭu: tāṅkaḷ virumpumpoḻutellām
   taṇṇīrai irattamāka māṟṟavum, maṇṇulakai ellāvakai vātaikaḷālum
   tākkavum avarkaḷukku atikāram uṇṭu.
   tive. 11:7 avarkaḷ cāṉṟu pakarntu muṭittapiṉ paṭukuḻiyiliruntu vauiyē
   varum vilaṅku avarkaḷōṭu pōr toṭuttu, avarkaḷai veṉṟu koṉṟuviṭum.
   tive. 11:8 cōtōm eṉavum ekiptu eṉavum uruvakamāka aḻaikkappaṭum
   ammānakariṉ terukkaḷil avarkaḷuṭaiya piṇaṅkaḷ kiṭakkum. aṅkē tāṉ
   avarkaḷiṉ āṇṭavar ciluvaiyil aṟaiyappaṭṭār.
   tive. 11:9 palvēṟu makkaḷiṉattār, kulattiṉar, moḻiyiṉar, nāṭṭiṉar
   mūṉṟarai nāḷ avarkaḷuṭaiya piṇaṅkaḷ aṅkuk kiṭakkak kāṇpārkaḷ: avaṟṟai
   aṭakkam ceyyaviṭamāṭṭārkaḷ.
   tive. 11:10 maṇṇulakil vāḻvōr avaṟṟaik kuṟittu mikavē makiḻntu
   tiḷaippar: oruvarukku oruvar aṉpaḷippukaḷ vaḻaṅkikkoḷvar: ēṉeṉil inta
   iraṇṭu iṟaivākkiṉarum maṇṇulakil vāḻvōrkkut tollai koṭuttiruntaṉar.
   tive. 11:11 anta mūṉṟarai nāḷukkup piṉ kaṭavuḷiṭamiruntu vanta
   uyirmūccu avaṟṟukkuḷ nuḻaintatum, avarkaḷ eḻuntu niṉṟārkaḷ. ataip
   pārttavarkaḷaip pēraccam āṭkoṇṭatu.
   tive. 11:12 appoḻutu viṇṇattiliruntu eḻunta ōr urattakural, '
   ivviṭattiṟku ēṟi vāruṅkaḷ ' eṉṟu taṅkaḷukkuc coṉṉatai anta
   iṟaivākkiṉarkaḷ iruvarum kēṭṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷuṭaiya pakaivarkaḷ pārttuk
   koṇṭirukka avarkaḷ mēkattiṉmītu viṇṇakattukkuc ceṉṟārkaḷ.
   tive. 11:13 annērattil perum nilanaṭukkam ēṟpaṭṭatu. nakariṉ pattil oru
   pakuti vīḻntatu. ataṉāl ēḻāyiram pēr iṟantaṉar. eñciṉōr, accam
   mēliṭṭavarkaḷāy viṇṇakak kaṭavuḷaip pōṟṟip pukaḻntaṉar.
   tive. 11:14 ivvāṟu iraṇṭāvatu kēṭu kaṭantu viṭṭatu: itō, mūṉṟāvatu kēṭu
   viraivil varavirukkiṟatu.
   tive. 11:15 piṉṉar ēḻāvatu vāṉatūtar ekkāḷam muḻakkiṉār. uṭaṉē
   viṇṇakattil uratta kural oṉṟu eḻuntatu: ' ulakiṉ āṭci urimai nam
   āṇṭavarukkum avaruṭaiya meciyāvukkum uriyatāyiṟṟu. avarē eṉṟeṉṟum āṭci
   purivār ' eṉṟu muḻakkam kēṭṭatu.
   tive. 11:16 kaṭavuḷ tirumuṉ taṅkaḷ ariyaṇaikaḷil amarntirunta
   irupattunāṉku mūpparkaḷum mukam kuppuṟa viḻuntu kaṭavuḷai
   vaṇaṅkiṉārkaḷ.
   tive. 11:17 ' kaṭavuḷākiya āṇṭavarē, ellām vallavarē, irukkiṉṟavarum
   iruntavarumāṉa umakku naṉṟi celuttukiṉṟōm: ēṉeṉil nīr umatu perum
   vallamaiyai vauippaṭutti āṭci celuttalāṉīr.
   tive. 11:18 vēṟṟiṉattār ciṉanteḻuntaṉar. umatu ciṉamum vauippaṭṭatu.
   iṟantōrukkut tīrppaḷikkavum um paṇiyāḷarkaḷākiya iṟaivākkiṉarkaḷ,
   iṟaimakkaḷ, umakku añcum ciṟiyōr, periyōr ākiya aṉaivarukkum kaimmāṟu
   aḷikkavum ulakai aḻippavarkaḷai aḻikkavum kālam vantu viṭṭatu ' eṉṟu
   pāṭiṉārkaḷ.
   tive. 11:19 appoḻutu viṇṇakattil kaṭavuḷiṉ kōvil tiṟakkappaṭṭatu. antak
   kōvilil uṭaṉpaṭikkaip pēḻai kāṇappaṭṭatu. miṉṉalum pēriraiccalum
   iṭimuḻakkamum nilanaṭukkamum kaṉatta kalmaḻaiyum uṇṭāyiṉa.
   -------------
   12-m atikāram
   tive. 12:1 vāṉil periyatōr aṭaiyāḷam tōṉṟiyatu: peṇ oruvar kāṇappaṭṭār:
   avar katiravaṉai āṭaiyāka aṇintiruntār: nilā avaruṭaiya kālaṭiyil
   iruntatu: avar paṉṉiru viṇmīṉkaḷait talaimītu cūṭiyiruntār.
   tive. 12:2 avar karuvuṟṟiruntār: pēṟukāla vētaṉaippaṭṭuk kaṭum
   tuyaruṭaṉ kataṟiṉār.
   tive. 12:3 vāṉil vēṟōr aṭaiyāḷamum tōṉṟiyatu: itō neruppumayamāṉa
   periya arakkap pāmpu oṉṟu kāṇappaṭṭatu. ataṟku ēḻu talaikaḷum pattuk
   kompukaḷum iruntaṉa. ataṉ talaikaḷil ēḻamaṇi muṭikaḷ iruntaṉa.
   tive. 12:4 atu taṉvālāl viṇmīṉkaḷiṉ mūṉṟil oru pakutiyai nilattiṉmītu
   iḻuttup pōṭṭatu. pēṟukāla vētaṉaiyilirunta appeṇ piḷḷai peṟṟavuṭaṉ atai
   viḻuṅkiviṭumāṟu arakkap pāmpu avarmuṉ niṉṟu koṇṭiruntatu.
   tive. 12:5 ellā nāṭukaḷaiyum iruppukkōl koṇṭu naṭattavirunta ōr āṇ
   kuḻantaiyai avar peṟṟeṭuttār. akkuḻantaiyō kaṭavuḷiṭam avaratu ariyaṇai
   irunta iṭattukkup paṟittuc cellappeṟṟatu.
   tive. 12:6 appeṇ pālainilattukku ōṭippōṉār: aṅku āyirattu irunūṟṟu
   aṟupatu nāḷ avaraip pēṇumāṟu kaṭavuḷ avarukkeṉa ōr iṭattai ēṟpāṭu
   ceytiruntār.
   tive. 12:7 piṉṉar viṇṇakattil pōr mūṇṭatu. mikkēlum avaruṭaiya
   tūtarkaḷum arakkap pāmpōṭu pōr toṭuttārkaḷ: arakkap pāmpum ataṉ
   tūtarkaḷum avarkaḷai etirttup pōriṭṭārkaḷ.
   tive. 12:8 arakkap pāmpu tōlviyuṟṟatu. viṇṇakattil ataṟkum ataṉ
   tūtarkaḷukkum iṭamē illātu pōyiṟṟu.
   tive. 12:9 apperiya arakkap pāmpu vauiyē taḷḷappaṭṭatu. alakai eṉṟum
   cāttāṉ eṉṟum aḻaikkap peṟṟa atuvē toṭakkattil tōṉṟiya pāmpu. ulaku
   muḻuvataiyum ēmāṟṟiya atu maṇṇulakukkut taḷḷappaṭṭatu: ataṉ tūtarkaḷum
   ataṉuṭaṉ vauiyē taḷḷappaṭṭārkaḷ.
   tive. 12:10 piṉpu viṇṇakattil olitta periyatoru kuralaik kēṭṭēṉ. atu
   coṉṉatu: ' itō, mīṭpu, vallamai, nam kaṭavuḷiṉ āṭci, avaruṭaiya
   meciyāviṉ atikāram ākiya aṉaittum vantuviṭṭaṉa. nam cakōtarar
   cakōtarikaḷ mītu kuṟṟam cumattiyavaṉ, nam kaṭavuḷ tirumuṉ allum pakalum
   avarkaḷmītu kuṟṟam cāṭṭiyavaṉ vauiyē taḷḷappaṭṭāṉ.
   tive. 12:11 āṭṭukkuṭṭi cintiya irattattālum tāṅkaḷ pakarnta cāṉṟālum
   avarkaḷ avaṉai veṉṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ uyirmītu ācai vaikkavillai:
   iṟakkavum tayaṅkavillai.
   tive. 12:12 itaṉporuṭṭu viṇṇulakē, atil kuṭiyiruppōrē, makiḻntu
   koṇṭāṭuṅkaḷ. maṇṇulakē, kaṭalē, aiyo. uṅkaḷukkuk kēṭu. taṉakkuc ciṟitu
   kālamē eñciyirukkiṟatu eṉpatai alakai aṟintuḷḷatu: ataṉāl kaṭuñ
   cīṟṟattuṭaṉ uṅkaḷiṭam vantuḷḷatu. '
   tive. 12:13 tāṉ maṇṇulakukkut taḷḷappaṭṭataik kaṇṭa arakkap pāmpu āṇ
   kuḻantaiyaip peṟṟeṭutta appeṇṇait turattic ceṉṟatu.
   tive. 12:14 āṉāl, appāmpiṭamiruntu tappittup pālainilattil avarukkeṉak
   kuṟikkapaṭṭirunta iṭattukkup paṟantu cellumāṟu, perum kaḻukiṉ iru
   ciṟakukaḷ avarukku aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. aṅku avar mūṉṟarai āṇṭuk kālam
   pēṇappaṭuvār.
   tive. 12:15 appeṇṇai veḷḷam aṭittuc cellumporuṭṭu, avar piṉṉāl appāmpu
   taṉ vāyiliruntu āṟupōlat taṇṇīr pāyntōṭac ceytatu.
   tive. 12:16 āṉāl nilam appeṇṇukkut tuṇai niṉṟatu. atu taṉ vāyait
   tiṟantu, arakkappāmpiṉ vāyiliruntu pāynta veḷḷattaik kuṭittuviṭṭatu.
   tive. 12:17 itaṉāl arakkappāmpu appeṇmītu ciṉaṅ koṇṭu, avaruṭaiya
   eñciya piḷḷaikaḷōṭu pōr toṭukkap puṟappaṭṭuc ceṉṟatu. avarkaḷ
   kaṭavuḷuṭaiya kaṭṭaḷaikaḷaik kaṭaippiṭittu iyēcuvukkuc cāṉṟu
   pakarntavarkaḷ.
   tive. 12:18 arakkappāmpu kaṭaṟkaraiyil niṉṟukoṇṭiruntatu.
   ---------
   13-m atikāram
   tive. 13:1 appoḻutu oru vilaṅku kaṭaliliruntu vauiyē varak kaṇṭēṉ.
   ataṟkup pattuk kompukaḷum ēḻu talaikaḷum iruntaṉa. ataṉ kompukaḷil
   pattu maṇimuṭikaḷum talaikaḷil kaṭavuḷaip paḻitturaikkum peyarkaḷum
   kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.
   tive. 13:2 nāṉ kaṇṭa anta vilaṅku ciṟuttaipōl iruntatu. ataṉ kālkaḷ
   karaṭiyiṉ kālkaḷpōṉṟum vāy ciṅkattiṉ vāypōṉṟum iruntaṉa. anta
   arakkappāmpu taṉ vallamaiyaiyum ariyaṇaiyaiyum pēratikārattaiyum ataṟku
   aḷittatu.
   tive. 13:3 anta vilaṅkiṉ talaikaḷuḷ oṉṟu uyirukkē ūṟu viḷaivikkakkūṭiya
   aḷavukkup paṭukāyappaṭṭiruntatupōl tōṉṟiyatu: āṉāl antap paṭukāyam
   kuṇamākiyiruntatu. maṇṇulaku muḻuvatum viyappuṟṟu avvilaṅkaip piṉ
   toṭarntatu.
   tive. 13:4 arakkappāmpu avvilaṅkukkut taṉ atikārattai aḷittiruntāl,
   makkaḷ appāmpai vaṇaṅkiṉārkaḷ: ' vilaṅkukku oppāṉavar yār? ataṉuṭaṉ
   pōriṭak kūṭiyavar yār? ' eṉṟu kūṟi avvilaṅkaiyum vaṇaṅkiṉārkaḷ.
   tive. 13:5 āṇavap pēccup pēcavum kaṭavuḷaip paḻitturaikkavum
   avvilaṅkukku aṉumati vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu: nāṟpattiraṇṭu mātam atu atikāram
   celutta viṭappaṭṭatu:
   tive. 13:6 kaṭavuḷaiyum avaratu peyaraiyum uṟaiviṭattaiyum viṇṇakattil
   kuṭiyiruppōraiyum paḻitturaikkat toṭaṅkiyatu.
   tive. 13:7 iṟaimakkaḷōṭu pōrtoṭukkavum avarkaḷai vellavum ataṟku
   aṉumati vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu: kulattiṉar, makkaḷiṉattiṉar, moḻiyiṉar,
   nāṭṭiṉar ākiya aṉaivarmītum ataṟku atikāram aḷikkappaṭṭatu.
   tive. 13:8 maṇṇulakil vāḻvōr aṉaivarum atai vaṇaṅkuvar. ivarkaḷ kolai
   ceyyappaṭṭa āṭṭukkuṭṭi vaittirukkum vāḻviṉ nūlil ulakam tōṉṟiyatu mutal
   peyar eḻutappaṭātōr. ivarkaḷ kolai ceyyappaṭṭa āṭṭukkuṭṭi vaittirukkum
   vāḻviṉ nūlil ulakam tōṉṟiyatu mutal peyar eḻutappaṭātōr.
   tive 13:9 kēṭkac cevi uṭaiyōr kēṭkaṭṭum:
   tive 13:10 "ciṟaiyilaṭappaṭa vēṇṭiyavar ciṟaiyilaṭappaṭuvar. vāḷāl
   kollappaṭa vēṇṭiyavar vāḷāl maṭivar." ākavē iṟaimakkaḷukku
   maṉavuṟutiyum nampikkaiyum tēvai.
   tive 13:11 piṉṉar maṟṟoru vilaṅku maṇṇiliruntu vauiyē varak kaṇṭēṉ.
   āṭṭukkāṭāviṉ kompukaḷaip pōṉṟu iru kompukaḷ ataṟku iruntaṉa. āṉāl atu
   arakkappāmpu pōṉṟu pēciyatu.
   tive 13:12 avvilaṅku mutalām vilaṅkiṉ muḻu atikārattaiyum ataṉ
   muṉṉilaiyil ceyalpaṭuttiyatu. uyirukkē ūṟu viḷaivikkakkūṭiya paṭu
   kāyattiṉiṉṟu kuṇam peṟṟirunta mutal vilaṅkai maṇṇulakum atil vāḻvōrum
   vaṇaṅkumpaṭi ceytatu.
   tive 13:13 atu periya aṭaiyāḷac ceyalkaḷ ceytatu. maṉitar pārkka
   viṇṇiliruntu maṇ mītu neruppu viḻumpaṭiyum ceytatu.
   tive 13:14 ivvāṟu mutal vilaṅkiṉ muṉṉilaiyil atu ceyyumpaṭi
   aṉumatikkappaṭṭirunta anta arum aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷāl maṇṇulakil vāḻvōrai
   ēmāṟṟiyatu. vāḷāl paṭukāyappaṭṭiruntum uyir vāḻnta avvilaṅkukkuc cilai
   oṉṟu ceyyumāṟu avarkaḷiṭam kūṟiyatu.
   tive 13:15 accilaiyaip pēcavaikkavum ataṉai vaṇaṅkātavarkaḷaik kolai
   ceyyavum ataṟku uyir koṭukkumāṟu iraṇṭām vilaṅkukku aṉumati
   vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu.
   tive 13:16 ciṟiyōr, periyōr, celvar, vaṟiyavar, urimaik kuṭimakkaḷ,
   aṭimaikaḷ ākiya aṉaivarum avaravar valak kaiyilāvatu neṟṟiyilāvatu kuṟi
   oṉṟu iṭṭukkoḷḷumāṟu ceytatu.
   tive 13:17 ivvāṟu anta vilaṅkiṉ peyaraiyō appeyarukkuriya eṇṇaiyō
   kuṟiyāka iṭṭukkoḷḷāta evarālum viṟkavō vāṅkavō muṭiyavillai.
   tive 13:18 itaip purintukoḷḷa ñāṉam tēvai. purintukoḷḷum āṟṟal koṇṭōr
   avvilaṅkukuriya eṇṇaik kaṇittu purintu koḷḷaṭṭum. anta eṇ ōr āḷaik
   kuṟikkum. atu aṟunūṟṟu aṟupattāṟu.
   -----------
   14-m atikāram
   tive 14:1 mēlum, cīyōṉ malaimītu āṭṭukkuṭṭi niṟkak kaṇṭēṉ. ataṉ
   peyaraiyum ataṉuṭaiya tantaiyiṉ peyaraiyum taṅkaḷatu neṟṟiyil
   poṟittirunta ōr ilaṭcattu nāṟpattu nāṉkāyirampēr ataṉuṭaṉ iruntaṉar.
   tive 14:2 piṉpu viṇṇakattiliruntu oru kuralaikkēṭṭēṉ. atu perum
   veḷḷattiṉ iraiccalpōlum pēriṭi muḻakkampōlum yāḻai mīṭṭuvōr eḻuppum
   icaipōlum olittatu.
   tive 14:3 anta ōr ilaṭcattu nāṟpattu nāṉkāyiram pērum ariyaṇai
   muṉṉilaiyil nāṉku uyikaḷukkum mūpparkaḷukkum muṉpākap putiyatoru
   pāṭalaip pāṭikkoṇṭiruntārkaḷ. maṇṇulakiliruntu vilai koṭuttu
   mīṭkappaṭṭa avarkaḷait tavira vēṟu yārālum antap pāṭalaik kaṟṟukkoḷḷa
   iyalavillai.
   tive 14:4 avarkaḷ peṇkaḷōṭu cērntu taṅkaḷaik kaṟaippaṭuttikkoḷḷāmal
   kaṟpaik kāttukkoṇṭavarkaḷ. āṭṭukkuṭṭi ceṉṟa iṭam eṅkum ataip piṉ
   toṭarntavarkaḷ. kaṭavuḷukkum āṭṭukkuṭṭikkum uriya mutaṟkaṉiyāka maṉita
   kulattiliruntu vilai koṭuttu mīṭkappaṭṭavarkaḷ.
   tive 14:5 avarkaḷatu vāyiṉiṉṟu poyyē vantatillai. ēṉeṉil avarkaḷ
   mācaṟṟavarkaḷ.
   tive 14:6 piṉpu vēṟoru vāṉatūtar naṭuvāṉil paṟantukoṇṭirukkak kaṇṭēṉ.
   avar maṇṇulakil vāḻvōrukku, atāvatu nāṭu, kulam, moḻi, makkaḷiṉam ākiya
   aṉaittukkum aṟivikkum poruṭṭu ekkālattukkum uriya naṟceytiyai
   vaittiruntār.
   tive 14:7 "kaṭavuḷukku añcuṅkaḷ. avaraip pōṟṟip pukaḻuṅkaḷ. ēṉeṉil avar
   tīrppaḷikkum nēram vantuviṭṭatu. maṇ, kaṭal, nīruṟṟukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟaip
   paṭaittavarai vaṇaṅkuṅkaḷ" eṉṟu avar uratta kuralil kūṟiṉār.
   tive 14:8 maṟṟoru vāṉatūtar avarait toṭarntu vantār. inta iraṇṭām
   tūtar, "vīḻntatu! parattaimai eṉṉum taṉ matuvai ellā nāṭṭiṉarum kuṭittu
   veṟikoḷḷac ceyta pāpilōṉ mānakar vīḻntatu!" eṉṟār.
   tive 14:9 vēṟoru vāṉatūtar avarkaḷait toṭarntu vantār. anta mūṉṟām
   vāṉatūtar uratta kuralil kūṟiyatu: "vilaṅkaiyum ataṉ cilaiyaiyum
   vaṇaṅki, taṅkaḷ neṟṟiyilō kaiyilō kuṟi iṭṭukkoṇṭōr aṉaivarum
   tive 14:10 kaṭavuḷiṉ cīṟṟam eṉṉum matuvai - avartam ciṉam eṉṉum
   kiṇṇattil kalappiṉṟi ūṟṟappaṭṭa anta matuvai - kuṭittē tīravēṇṭum.
   avarkaḷ tūya vāṉatūtar muṉṉilaiyilum āṭṭukkuṭṭiyiṉ muṉṉilaiyilum
   neruppālum kantakattālum vataikkappaṭuvārkaḷ.
   tive 14:11 avarkaḷai vataitta neruppiliruntu eḻunta pukai eṉṟeṉṟum mēlē
   eḻunta vaṇṇam uḷḷatu. anta vilaṅkaiyum ataṉ cilaiyaiyum vaṇaṅkuvōrukkum
   ataṉuṭaiya peyaraik kuṟiyāka iṭṭukkoṇṭōrukkum allum pakalum ōyvē irātu.
   tive 14:12 ākavē kaṭavuḷuṭaiya kaṭṭaḷaikaḷaik kaṭaippiṭittu, iyēcuviṉ
   mītu nampikkai koṇṭirukkum iṟaimakkaḷukku maṉavuṟuti tēvai."
   tive 14:13 piṉpu viṇṇakattiliruntu oru kuralaik kēṭṭēṉ: "'itu mutal
   āṇṭavarōṭu iṇainta nilaiyil iṟappōr pēṟupeṟṟōr' eṉa eḻutu" eṉṟu atu
   olittatu. ataṟkut tūya āviyār, "ām, avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ uḻaippiliruntu ōyvu
   peṟuvārkaḷ. ēṉeṉil avarkaḷiṉ ceyalkaḷ avarkaḷaip piṉ toṭarum" eṉṟu
   kūṟiṉār.
   tive 14:14 piṉpu oru veṇ mēkattaik kaṇṭēṉ. ataṉmītu māṉiṭa
   makaṉaippōṉṟa oruvar vīṟṟiruntār. avaratu talaiyil poṉmuṭiyum kaiyil
   kūrmaiyāṉa arivāḷum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.
   tive 14:15 maṟṟoru vāṉatūtar kōvililiruntu vauiyē vantu, mēkattiṉmītu
   vīṟṟiruntavarai nōkki, "umatu arivāḷai eṭuttu aṟuvaṭai ceyyum. ēṉeṉil
   aṟuvaṭaikkālam vantuviṭṭatu. maṇṇulakam eṉṉum payir muṟṟiviṭṭatu" eṉṟu
   uratta kuralil kattiṉār.
   tive 14:16 uṭaṉē mēkattiṉmītu vīṟṟiruntavar maṇṇulakameṅkum tamatu
   arivāḷai vīci aṟuvaṭai ceytār.
   tive 14:17 maṟṟoru vāṉatūtarum viṇṇakattil uḷḷa kōvililiruntu vauiyē
   vantār. avariṭamum kūrmaiyāṉa arivāḷ oṉṟu iruntatu.
   tive 14:18 neruppiṉmēl atikāram koṇṭirunta iṉṉumoru vāṉatūtar
   palipīṭattiliruntu vauiyē vantār. avar kūrmaiyāṉa arivāḷai
   vaittiruntavariṭam, "umatu kūrmaiyāṉa arivāḷai eṭuttu maṇṇulakiṉ
   tirāṭcaik kulaikaḷai aṟuttuc cērttiṭum. ēṉeṉil tirāṭcai kaṉintuviṭṭatu"
   eṉṟu uratta kuralil kūṟiṉār.
   tive 14:19 ākavē anta vāṉatūtar maṇṇulakiṉmītu tam arivāḷai vīci
   maṇṇulakiṉ tirāṭcaik kulaikaḷai aṟuttuc cērttār. kaṭavuḷiṉ cīṟṟam eṉṉum
   periya piḻivukkuḻiyil avaṟṟaip pōṭṭār. nakarukku vauiyē irunta antap
   piḻivukkuḻiyiliruntu iratta veḷḷam ēṟattāḻa iraṇṭu mīṭṭar āḻam, munnūṟu
   kilō mīṭṭar tolaikkup pāyntōṭiyatu.
   -----------
   15-m atikāram
   tive 15:1 piṉpu periyatum viyappukkuriyatumāṉa maṟṟōr aṭaiyāḷattai
   viṇṇakattil kaṇṭēṉ. ēḻu vāṉatūtarkaḷ ēḻu vātaikaḷaik koṇṭiruntārkaḷ.
   iṟutiyāṉa anta vātaikaḷōṭu kaṭavuḷiṉ cīṟṟam muṟṟilum taṇiyum.
   tive 15:2 neruppōṭu kalanta kaṇṇāṭik kaṭalpōṉṟa oṉṟaiyum kaṇṭēṉ.
   toṭarntu, vilaṅkiṉmītum ataṉ cilaimītum eṇṇāl kuṟikkappeṟṟa anta
   āḷmītum veṟṟi peṟṟavarkaḷ, kaṭavuḷ koṭuttirunta yāḻkaḷai ēntiya vaṇṇam
   kaṇṇāṭikkaṭal arukē niṉṟu koṇṭirukkak kaṇṭēṉ.
   tive 15:3 avarkaḷ kaṭavuḷiṉ paṇiyāḷarāṉa mōcēyiṉ pāṭalaiyum
   āṭṭukkuṭṭiyiṉ pāṭalaiyum pāṭik koṇṭiruntārkaḷ: "kaṭavuḷākiya āṇṭavarē,
   ellām vallavarē, um ceyalkaḷ periyaṉa, viyappukkuriyaṉa.
   makakaḷiṉaṅkaḷiṉ maṉṉarē, um vaḻikaḷ nēriyavai, uṇmaiyuḷḷavai.
   tive 15:4 āṇṭavarē, umakku añcātavar yār? umatu peyaraip pōṟṟi
   pukaḻātār yār? nīr oruvarē tūyavar, ellā makkaḷiṉaṅkaḷum um tirumuṉ
   vantu vaṇaṅkum. ēṉeṉil um nītic ceyalkaḷ vauippaṭaiyāyiṉa."
   tive 15:5 itaṉ piṉ viṇṇakattil uḷḷa kōvil, atāvatu cantippuk kūṭāram
   tiṟakkak kaṇṭēṉ.
   tive 15:6 appoḻutu ēḻu vātaikaḷaik koṇṭirunta ēḻu vāṉatūtarkaḷum
   kōvililiruntu vauiyē vantārkaḷ. avarkaḷ tūymaiyāṉa, paḷapaḷappāṉa
   melliya āṭaiyum mārpil poṉpaṭṭaiyum aṇintiruntārkaḷ.
   tive 15:7 anta nāṉku uyirkaḷuḷ oṉṟu eṉṟeṉṟum vāḻum kaṭavuḷiṉ cīṟṟattāl
   niṟainta ēḻu poṉ kiṇṇaṅkaḷai anta ēḻu vāṉa tūtarkaḷukkum aḷittatu.
   tive 15:8 kaṭavuḷiṉ māṭciyum vallamaiyum kōvilaip pukaiyāl nirappiṉa.
   ataṉāl anta ēḻu tūtarkaḷum koṇṭirunta ēḻu vātaikaḷum muṭivuṟumvarai
   oruvarum kōviluḷ nuḻaiya muṭiyavillai.
   -----------
   16-m atikāram
   tive 16:1 kōvililiruntu olitta oru perum kuralaik kēṭṭēṉ. "puṟappaṭṭuc
   celluṅkaḷ. ēḻu kiṇṇaṅkaḷilum irunta kaṭavuḷiṉ cīṟṟattai maṇṇulakiṉ mītu
   ūṟṟuṅkaḷ" eṉṟu anta ēḻu vāṉatūtarkaḷukkum atu coṉṉatu.
   tive 16:2 uṭaṉē mutalām vāṉatūtar ceṉṟu, tam kiṇṇattil iruntatai
   maṇṇulakiṉmītu ūṟṟiṉār. vilaṅkukkuriya kuṟiyai iṭṭukkoṇṭu ataṉ cilaiyai
   vaṇaṅki vanta maṉitarmītu koṭiya tuṉpam tarakkūṭiya puṇkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa.
   tive 16:3 piṉ iraṇṭām vāṉatūtar tam kiṇṇattil iruntataik kaṭalil
   ūṟṟiṉār. appoḻutu atu iṟantōriṉ irattam pōṉṟu māṟiyatu. uṭaṉē kaṭalvāḻ
   uyiriṉaṅkaḷ ellām maṭintaṉa.
   tive 16:4 mūṉṟām vāṉatūtar tam kiṇṇattil iruntatai āṟukaḷmītum
   nīruṟṟukaḷmītum ūṟṟiṉār. uṭaṉē avaiyum irattampōla māṟiṉa.
   tive 16:5 nīrnilaikaḷ mītu atikāram koṇṭirunta vāṉatūtar piṉvarumāṟu
   collak kēṭṭēṉ: "irukkiṉṟavarum iruntavarumāṉa tūyavarē, ittīrppukaḷai
   vaḻaṅkum nīr nītiyuḷḷavar.
   tive 16:6 iṟaimakkaḷuṭaiyavum iṟaivākkiṉarkaḷuṭaiyavum irattattaic
   cintiya māṉiṭarukku nīr irattattaiyē koṭuttīr. itu avarkaḷukkut takunta
   taṇṭaṉaiyē."
   tive 16:7 appoḻutu palipīṭattiliruntu nāṉ kēṭṭa kural, "ām,
   kaṭavuḷākiya āṇṭavarē, ellām vallavarē, um tīrppukaḷ uṇmaiyuḷḷavai,
   nītiyāṉavai" eṉṟatu.
   tive 16:8 nāṉkām vāṉatūtar tam kiṇṇattil iruntataik katiravaṉmītu
   ūṟṟiṉār. ataṉāl maṉitarai neruppāy erikkum vallamaiyai atu peṟṟatu.
   tive 16:9 uṭaṉē maṉitar kaṭum veppattāl erikkappaṭṭaṉar. inta vātaikaḷ
   mītu atikāram koṇṭirunta kaṭavuḷiṉ peyarai avarkaḷ paḻittārkaḷē tavira,
   maṉam māṟi avaraip pōṟṟip pukaḻavillai.
   tive 16:10 aintām vāṉatūtar tam kiṇṇattil iruntatai vilaṅku vīṟṟirunta
   ariyaṇaimītu ūṟṟiṉār. uṭaṉē ataṉ aracai iruḷ kavvikkoṇṭatu. tuṉpam
   tāṅka muṭiyātavarkaḷāy maṉitar taṅkaḷ nāvaik kaṭittuk koṇṭaṉar.
   tive 16:11 taṅkaḷ tuṉpattaiyum puṇkaḷaiyum muṉṉiṭṭu viṇṇakak kaṭavuḷaip
   paḻittārkaḷē tavira, taṅkaḷ ceyalkaḷaiviṭṭu maṉam māṟavillai.
   tive 16:12 āṟām vāṉatūtar tam kiṇṇattil iruntatai yūppiratīcu pērāṟṟil
   ūṟṟiṉār. uṭaṉē ataṉ taṇṇīr vaṟṟippōṉatu. ataṉāl kīḻai nāṭṭu maṉṉarkaḷ
   payaṉpaṭutta pātai uṇṭāyiṟṟu.
   tive 16:13 arakkap pāmpiṉiṉ vāyiṉiṉṟum pōli iṟaivākkiṉar vāyiṉiṉṟum
   tavaḷai pōṉṟa mūṉṟu tīya āvikaḷ vauivarak kaṇṭēṉ.
   tive 16:14 avai arum aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷ puriyum pēykaḷiṉ āvikaḷ. ellā valla
   kaṭavuḷiṉ perum nāḷil pōr purintiṭa ulaku aṉaittiṉ aracarkaḷai oṉṟu
   kūṭṭa avai puṟappaṭṭac ceṉṟaṉa.
   tive 16:15 "itō! nāṉ tiruṭaṉaip pōla varukiṟēṉ. tāṅkaḷ āṭai iṉṟi
   naṭappataiyum piṟanta mēṉiyāy iruppataiyum piṟar pārttiṭātavāṟu
   taṅkaḷiṉ āṭaikaḷai āyattamāka vaittiruppōrum viḻippāy iruppōrum
   pēṟupeṟṟōr."
   tive 16:16 epirēya moḻiyil 'arumakatōṉ' eṉappaṭum iṭattil anta āvikaḷ
   aracarkaḷai oṉṟu kūṭṭiṉa.
   tive 16:17 ēḻām vāṉatūtar tam kiṇṇattil iruntatai vāṉvauiyil ūṟṟiṉār.
   appoḻutu kōviliṉ ariyaṇaiyiliruntu, "ellām niṟaivēṟṟappaṭṭāyiṟṟu" eṉṟu
   oru perum kural olittatu.
   tive 16:18 uṭaṉē miṉṉalum iraiccalum iṭimuḻakkamum uṇṭāyiṉa. periyatoru
   nilanaṭukkamum ēṟpaṭṭatu. maṉitar maṇṇil tōṉṟiya nāḷmutal ittuṇai
   periya nilanaṭukkam ēṟpaṭṭatē illai.
   tive 16:19 anta mānakar mūṉṟu pākaṅkaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭatu. maṟṟa
   nāṭukaḷiṉ nakarkaḷum vīḻntaṉa. kaṭavuḷ pāpilōṉ mānakarai niṉaivil koṇṭu
   tam kaṭum cīṟṟam eṉṟa matu nirampiya kiṇṇattai ataṟkuk kuṭikkak
   koṭuttār.
   tive 16:20 tīvukaḷellām maṟaintupōyiṉa. malaikaḷum kāṇappaṭavillai.
   tive 16:21 kalmaḻai peruṅkaṟkaḷāka viṇṇiliruntu makkaḷmītu peytatu.
   kalmaḻaiyāl ēṟpaṭṭa ivvātai mikak koṭiyatāy iruntatāl, makkaḷ
   kaṭavuḷaip paḻitturaittārkaḷ.
   --------
   17-m atikāram
   tive 17:1 ēḻu kiṇṇaṅkaḷaik koṇṭirunta ēḻu vāṉa tūtarkaḷuḷ oruvar vantu
   eṉṉōṭu pēci, "vā, nīrttiraḷmēl amarntirukkum pēr pōṉa vilaimakaḷukku
   varavirukkum taṇṭaṉaiyai uṉakkuk kāṭṭuvēṉ.
   tive 17:2 maṇṇulakiṉ aracarkaḷ avaḷōṭu parattaimaiyil īṭupaṭṭārkaḷ.
   maṇṇulakil vāḻvōr avaḷatu parattaimai eṉṉum matuviṉāl
   veṟikoṇṭirukkiṟārkaḷ" eṉṟār.
   tive 17:3 appoḻutu tūya āviyāl āṭkoḷḷappeṟṟa eṉṉai anta vāṉatūtar
   pālainilattukkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟār. aṅkē karuñcivappu vilaṅkiṉmītu
   amarntirunta oru peṇṇaik kaṇṭēṉ. avvilaṅkiṉ uṭal muḻutum kaṭavuḷaip
   paḻitturaikkum peyarkaḷ niṟaintiruntaṉa. ataṟku ēḻu talaikaḷum pattuk
   kompukaḷum iruntaṉa.
   tive 17:4 appeṇ cenniṟa karuñcivappu āṭaikaḷai aṇintiruntāḷ. poṉ,
   vilaiyuyarnta kal, muttu ākiyavaṟṟāl aṇi ceyyappaṭṭiruntāḷ. avaḷatu
   parattaimaiyiṉ aruvaruppum aḻukkum niṟainta poṉ kiṇṇam avaḷatu kaiyil
   iruntatu.
   tive 17:5 maṟaiporuḷ koṇṭa peyar oṉṟu avaḷatu neṟṟiyil
   eḻutappaṭṭiruntatu. "pāpilōṉ mānakar vilaimakaḷirukkum maṇṇulakiṉ
   aruvaruppukaḷ aṉaittukkumē tāy" eṉpatē ataṉ poruḷ.
   tive 17:6 appeṇ iṟaimakkaḷiṉ irattattaiyum iyēcuviṉ cāṭcikaḷuṭaiya
   irattattaiyum kuṭittu veṟikoṇṭirukkak kaṇṭēṉ. nāṉ avaḷaik kaṇṭapōtu
   perum viyappil āḻntēṉ.
   tive 17:7 ataṟku anta vāṉatūtar eṉṉiṭam kūṟiyatu: "nī viyappu aṭaivatu
   ēṉ? appeṇṇaippaṟṟiya maṟaiporuḷaiyum, ēḻu talaikaḷum pattuk kompukaḷum
   koṇṭatāy avaḷaic cumantu cellum vilaṅkiṉ maṟaiporuḷaiyum uṉakkuc
   colkiṟēṉ.
   tive 17:8 nī kaṇṭa vilaṅku muṉpu uyirōṭu iruntatu. ippōtu illai.
   paṭukuḻiyiliruntu atu ēṟivaravirukkiṟatu. āṉāl aḻintuviṭum. ulakam
   tōṉṟiyatumutal vāḻviṉ nūlil peyar eḻutappaṭāta maṇṇulakuvāḻ makkaḷ
   aṉaivarum anta vilaṅkaik kaṇṭu viyappu aṭaivārkaḷ. ēṉeṉil atu muṉpu
   uyirōṭu illai. āṉāl mīṇṭum uyir peṟṟu varum.
   tive 17:9 itaip purintukoḷḷa ñāṉam tēvaippaṭukiṟatu. anta ēḻu
   talaikaḷum appeṇ amarntirukkum ēḻu malaikaḷaik kuṟikkum. ēḻu
   aracarkaḷaiyum kuṟikkum.
   tive 17:10 inta aracarkaḷuḷ aivar vīḻcciyuṟṟavar. ippōtu oruvar āṭci
   celuttukiṟār. iṉṉoruvar iṉṉum tōṉṟavillai. avar tōṉṟic ciṟitu kālamē
   āṭci puriya muṭiyum.
   tive 17:11 muṉpu uyirōṭu iruntu ippōtu illāta anta vilaṅku eṭṭāvatu
   aracaraik kuṟikkum. anta ēḻu aracarkaḷuḷ oruvarāṉa avarum
   aḻintuviṭuvār.
   tive 17:12 nī kaṇṭa pattuk kompukaḷum pattu aracarkaḷaik kuṟikkum.
   avarkaḷ iṉṉum āṭciyurimaip peṟavillai. āṉāl, avarkaḷ vilaṅkōṭu cērntu
   oru maṇi aḷavu aracāḷa atikāram peṟuvārkaḷ.
   tive 17:13 avarkaḷ oru maṉappaṭṭavarāyt taṅkaḷ vallamaiyaiyum
   atikārattaiyum avvilaṅkiṭam oppaṭaittārkaḷ.
   tive 17:14 avarkaḷ āṭṭukkuṭṭiyōṭu pōr purivārkaḷ. āṉāl, atu avarkaḷai
   veṉṟuviṭum. kaṭavuḷāl aḻaikkappeṟṟu, tērnteṭukkap peṟṟu uṇmai
   uḷḷavarkaḷāy āṭṭukkuṭṭiyōṭu iruppavarkaḷum veṟṟi koḷvārkaḷ. ēṉeṉil
   āṭṭuk kuṭṭi āṇṭavarukkellām āṇṭavar. aracarukkellām aracar."
   tive 17:15 vāṉatūtar toṭarntu eṉṉiṭam coṉṉatu: "anta vilaimakaḷ
   nīrttiraḷmēl amarntirukka ī kaṇṭāy. anta nīrttiraḷ palvēṟu iṉattiṉar,
   makkaḷ kūṭṭattiṉar, nāṭṭiṉar, moḻiyiṉar ākiyōraik kuṟikkum.
   tive 17:16 nī kaṇṭa pattuk kompukaḷum vilaṅkum anta vilaimakaḷmītu
   veṟuppuk koṇṭu, avaḷiṭamiruntu aṉaittaiyum paṟittuviṭṭu, avaḷaip
   piṟantamēṉi ākkum. avaḷatu cataiyait tiṉṟu, avaḷai neruppiṉāl
   cuṭṭerikkum.
   tive 17:17 ēṉeṉil kaṭavuḷ tamatu nōkkattai niṟaivēṟṟavē annāṭṭiṉariṉ
   uḷḷaṅkaḷait tūṇṭiviṭṭār. avaratu vākku niṟaivēṟumvarai, avarkaḷ
   orumaṉappaṭṭavarāyt taṅkaḷatu āṭciyai vilaṅkiṭam oppaṭaittatum atē
   kāraṇattiṉāltāṉ.
   tive 17:18 nī kaṇṭa peṇ maṇṇulaka aracarkaḷmītu āṭci celuttum mānakar
   ākum."
   ----------
   18-m atikāram
   tive 18:1 itaṉpiṉ vēṟoru vāṉatūtar viṇṇakattiliruntu iṟaṅkivarak
   kaṇṭēṉ. mikunta atikāram koṇṭirunta avaruṭaiya māṭciyāl maṇṇakam
   oḷirntatu.
   tive 18:2 avar vallamaiyōṭu kuraleḻuppip piṉvarumāṟu kattiṉār:
   "vīḻnttu!vīḻntatu pāpilōṉ mānakar! avaḷ pēykaḷiṉ uṟaiviṭamāka, aṉaittut
   tīya āvikaḷiṉ patuṅkiṭamāka, tūymaiyaṟṟa paṟavaikaḷ aṉaittiṉ
   pukaliṭamāka, tūymaiyaṟṟa veṟukkattakka vilaṅkukaḷiṉ iruppiṭammāka
   māṟiviṭṭāḷ.
   tive 18:3 avvilaimakaḷiṉ kāmaveṟi eṉṉum matuvai ellā nāṭṭiṉarum
   kuṭittaṉar. maṇṇulaka aracarkaḷ avaḷōṭu parattamaiyil īṭupaṭṭārkaḷ.
   ulakiṉ vaṇikarkaḷ avaḷuṭaiya vaḷaṅkaḷāl celvarkaḷ āṉārkaḷ."
   tive 18:4 piṉṉar viṇṇiliruntu iṉṉoru kuralaik kēṭṭēṉ. atu coṉṉatu: eṉ
   makkaḷē, annakaraiviṭṭu vauiyēṟuṅkaḷ, avaḷuṭaiya pāvaṅkaḷil paṅku
   koḷḷātirukkavum avaḷukku nēriṭum vātaikaḷukku uṭpaṭātirukkavum vauiyē
   pōyviṭuṅkaḷ.
   tive 18:5 avaḷiṉ pāvaṅkaḷ vāṉaittoṭum aḷavukkuk kuvintuḷḷaṉa. kaṭavuḷ
   avaḷiṉ kuṟṟaṅkaḷai niṉaivil koṇṭuḷḷār.
   tive 18:6 avaḷ uṅkaḷai naṭattiyavāṟē nīṅkaḷum avaḷai naṭattuṅkaḷ.
   avaḷuṭaiya ceyalkaḷukku ēṟpa iraṭṭippākat tiruppik koṭuṅkaḷ.
   tive 18:7 avaḷ taṉṉaiyē perumaippaṭutti iṉpam tuyttu vāḻntataṟku ēṟpa
   avaḷ vētaṉaiyuṟṟut tuyaraṭaiyac ceyyuṅkaḷ. ēṉeṉil, 'nāṉ araciyāka
   vīṟṟirukkiṟēṉ. nāṉ kaimpeṇ alla. nāṉ orupōtum tuyaruṟēṉ' eṉṟu avaḷ taṉ
   uḷḷattil collikkoṇṭāḷ.
   tive 18:8 itaṉporuṭṭuc cāvu, tuyaram, pañcam ākiya vātaikaḷ orē nāḷil
   avaḷmītu vantu viḻum. neruppu avaḷaic cuṭṭerittuviṭum. ēṉeṉil avaḷukkut
   tīrppu vaḻaṅkum āṇṭavarākiya kaṭavuḷ valimai vāyntavar."
   tive 18:9 annakarōṭu parattaimaiyil īṭupaṭṭu iṉpam tuyttu vāḻnta
   maṇṇulaka aracarkaḷ avaḷ eriyumpōtu eḻum pukaiyaip pārttu aḻutu
   māraṭittup pulampuvārkaḷ.
   tive 18:10 avaḷ paṭum vētaṉaikaḷaik kaṇṭu añcit tolaiyil niṉṟu koṇṭu,
   "aiyō! mānakarē nī kēṭuṟṟāyē! antō! valimai vāynta pāpilōṉē uṉakkuk
   kēṭu vantatē! orē maṇi nērattil uṉakkut tīrppu vantuviṭṭatē." eṉpārkaḷ.
   tive 18:11 maṇṇaka vaṇikarkaḷum avaḷai niṉaittu aḻutu pulampuvārkaḷ.
   ēṉeṉil avarkaḷuṭaiya carakkukaḷai iṉi vāṅkuvār evarum ilar.
   tive 18:12 poṉ, veḷḷi, vilaiyuyarnta kal, muttukaḷ, vilaiyuyarnta
   melliya āṭai, karuñcivappu āṭai, paṭṭāṭai, cenniṟa āṭai, palavakai
   maṇam vīcum marakkaṭṭaikaḷ, tantattiṉālāṉa palavakaip poruḷkaḷ,
   vilaiyuyarnta maram, veṇkalam, irumpu, calavaikkal ākiyavaṟṟālāṉa
   poruḷkaḷ,
   tive 18:13 ilavaṅkam, naṟumaṇap poruḷkaḷ, tūpa vakaikaḷ, naṟumaṇat
   tailam, cāmpirāṇi, tirāṭcai matu, eṇṇey, uyarraka māvu, kōtumai,
   āṭumāṭukaḷ, kutiraikaḷ, tērkaḷ, aṭimaikaḷ ākiya maṉita uyirkaḷ
   ākiyavaṟṟaiyellām vāṅka evarum ilar.
   tive 18:14 "nī virumpiya kaṉikaḷ uṉṉaiviṭṭu akaṉṟupōyiṉa. uṉ miṉukku,
   pakaṭṭu ellām oḻintupōyiṉa. iṉi yārum avaṟṟaik kāṇap pōvatillai"
   eṉpārkaḷ.
   tive 18:15 iccarakkukaḷaik koṇṭu avaḷōṭu vāṇikam ceytu celvam
   tiraṭṭiyavarkaḷ avaḷatu vētaṉaiyaik kaṇṭu añci, tolaiyilēyē niṉṟa
   vaṇṇam aḻutu pulampuvārkaḷ.
   tive 18:16 "aiyō, mānakarē, nī kēṭuṟṟāyē! vilaiyuyarnta melliya āṭaiyum
   cenniṟa karuñcivappu uṭaiyum aṇintu, poṉ, vilaiyuyarnta kal, muttukaḷāl
   aṇiceytu koṇṭavaḷē! antō! uṉakkuk kēṭu vantatē!
   tive 18:17 ivvaḷavu celvamum orē maṇi nērattil pāḻāyp pōyviṭṭatē"
   eṉpārkaḷ. kappal talaivarkaḷ, kaṭal payaṇikaḷ, kappalōṭṭikaḷ, kaṭal
   vaṇikarkaḷ ākiya aṉaivarum tolaiyilēyē niṉṟārkaḷ.
   tive 18:18 avaḷ erintapōtu eḻunta pukaiyaip pārttu "immānakarukku
   iṇaiyāṉa nakar uṇṭō!" eṉṟu kataṟiṉārkaḷ.
   tive 18:19 avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ talaimēl puḻutiyai vārippōṭṭukkoṇṭu aḻutu
   pulampiṉārkaḷ: "aiyō, mānakarē, nī kēṭuṟṟāyē! kaṭalil kappalōṭṭiya
   aṉaivaraiyum taṉ celvacceḻippāl celvarākkiya nī orē maṇi nērattil
   pāḻaṭaintuviṭṭāyē!" eṉṟu kataṟiṉārkaḷ.
   tive 18:20 "viṇṇakamē, iṟaimakkaḷē, tiruttūtarkaḷē, iṟaivākkiṉarkaḷē,
   avaḷaimuṉṉiṭṭu makiḻntu koṇṭāṭuṅkaḷ. kaṭavuḷ uṅkaḷ cārpāka avaḷukkut
   taṇṭaṉait tīrppu vaḻaṅkiviṭṭār.
   tive 18:21 piṉṉar valimai vāynta oru vāṉatūtar periya entirakkal pōṉṟa
   oru kallait tūkkik kaṭalil eṟintu piṉvarumāṟ kūṟiṉār: "pāpilōṉ
   mānakarē, nī ivvāṟ vīci eṟiyappaṭuvāy. nī irunta iṭam teriyāmal aḻintu
   viṭuvāy.
   tive 18:22 yāḻai mīṭṭuvōr, pāṭakar, kuḻal ūtuvōr, ekkāḷam muḻakkuvōr
   ākiyōr eḻuppum icai iṉi uṉ naṭuvē eḻavē eḻātu. toḻil ceyyum kaiviṉaiñar
   aṉaivarum iṉi uṉ naṭuvē kuṭiyirukkavē māṭṭārkaḷ. entirakkal eḻuppum oli
   iṉi uṉ naṭuvē eḻavē eḻātu.
   tive 18:23 viḷakkiṉ oḷi iṉi uṉṉakattē oḷiravē oḷirātu. maṇamakkaḷiṉ
   maṅkala oli iṉi uṉṉakattē eḻavē eḻātu. ēṉeṉil uṉ vaṇikarkaḷ maṇṇulakil
   peruṅkuṭi makkaḷāy viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. uṉ pillicūṉiyamum ellā nāṭukaḷaiyum
   ēmāṟṟiviṭṭatu.
   tive 18:24 iṟaivākkiṉarkaḷ, iṟaimakkaḷiṉ irattakkaṟaiyum, ēṉ,
   maṇṇulakil kollappaṭṭa aṉaivaruṭaiya irattakkaṟaiyumē avaḷiṭam
   kāṇappaṭṭatu."
   ----------
   19-m atikāram
   tive 19:1 itaṉpiṉ peruntiraḷāṉa makkaḷiṉ kūccalpōṉṟa oli viṇṇakattil
   eḻak kēṭṭēṉ. atu piṉvarumāṟu muḻaṅkiyatu: "allēlūyā! mīṭpum māṭciyum
   vallamaiyum nam kaṭavuḷukē uriyaṉa.
   tive 19:2 ēṉeṉil avaruṭaiya tīrppukaḷ uṇmai uḷḷavai, nītiyāṉavai. tam
   parattaimaiyāl maṇṇulakai aḻivukkuṭpaṭuttiya pērpōṉa anta
   vilaimakaḷukku avar tīrppu vaḻaṅkiṉār. tam paṇiyāḷarkaḷaik koṉṟataṟkāka
   avaḷaip paḻivāṅkiṉār."
   tive 19:3 mīṇṭum anta makkaḷ, "allēlūyā! anta nakar naṭuviliruntu pukai
   eṉṟeṉṟum mēlē eḻunta vaṇṇam uḷḷatu" eṉṟārkaḷ.
   tive 19:4 anta irupattu nāṉku mūpparkaḷum nāṉku uyirkaḷum ariyaṇaiyil
   vīṟṟirunta kaṭavuḷmuṉ viḻuntu vaṇaṅki, "āmeṉ, allēlūyā" eṉṟu
   pāṭiṉārkaḷ.
   tive 19:5 ariyaṇaiyiliruntu eḻunta oru kural, "kaṭavuḷiṉ paṇiyāḷarkaḷe,
   avarukku añci naṭappavarkaḷē, ciṟiyōrkaḷē, periyōrkaḷē, nīṅkaḷ
   aṉaivarum nam kaṭavuḷaip pukaḻuṅkaḷ" eṉṟu olittatu.
   tive 19:6 piṉṉar peruntiraḷāṉa makkaḷiṉ kūccalpōlum perum veḷḷattiṉ
   iraiccalpōlum pēriṭi muḻakkam pōlum eḻunta pēroliyaik kēṭṭēṉ. atu
   coṉṉatu: "allēlūyā! nam kaṭavuḷākiya āṇṭavar, ellām vallavar. avar āṭci
   celuttukiṟār.
   tive 19:7 eṉavē makiḻvōm. pēruvakaiyuṭaṉ avaraip pōṟṟip pukaḻvōm.
   ēṉeṉil āṭṭukkuṭṭiyiṉ tirumaṇa viḻā vantu viṭṭatu. maṇamakaḷum viḻāvukku
   āyattamāy irukkiṟār.
   tive 19:8 maṇamakaḷ aṇiyumāṟu paḷapaḷappāṉa, tūymaiyāṉa, vilaiyuyarnta
   melliya āṭai avarukku aḷikkappaṭṭatu. anta āṭai iṟaimakkaḷiṉ nītic
   ceyalkaḷē."
   tive 19:9 anta vāṉatūtar eṉṉiṭam, "'āṭṭukkuṭṭiyiṉ tirumaṇa viruntukku
   aḻaikkappeṟṟōr pēṟupeṟṟōr' eṉa eḻutu" eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. toṭarntu, "ivai
   kaṭavuḷiṉ uṇmaiyuḷḷa coṟkaḷ" eṉṟu coṉṉār.
   tive 19:10 nāṉ vāṉatūtarai vaṇaṅkum poruṭṭu avaratu kālaṭiyil viḻuntēṉ.
   āṉāl avarō eṉṉiṭam, "vēṇṭām, iyēcuvukkuc cāṉṟu pakarnta" uṉakkum uṉ
   uṭaṉpiṟappukaḷukkum nāṉ uṭaṉpaṇiyāḷaṉē. kaṭavuḷai maṭṭumē nī vaṇaṅka
   vēṇṭum" eṉṟār. ēṉeṉil iyēcu pakarnta cāṉṟē iṟaivākkukaḷukku uyir mūccu.
   tive 19:11 piṉṉar nāṉ viṇṇakam tiṟantirukkak kaṇṭēṉ. aṅkē oru
   veṇkutirai kāṇappaṭṭatu. ataṉmēl oruvar amarntiruntār.
   'nampikkaikkuriyavar, uṇmaiyuḷḷavar' eṉpatu avaruṭaiya peyar. avar
   nītiyōṭu tīrppaḷittup pōr toṭuppār.
   tive 19:12 avaruṭaiya kaṇkaḷ tīppiḻampu pōlat teṉpaṭṭaṉa. avaratu
   talaimēl pala maṇimuṭikaḷ iruntaṉa. avaraittavira vēṟu evarukkum
   terintirāta peyar oṉṟu avar mītu eḻutappaṭṭiruntatu.
   tive 19:13 irattam tōynta āṭaiyai avar aṇintiruntār. 'kaṭavuḷiṉ vākku'
   eṉpatu avaratu peyar.
   tive 19:14 veṇmaiyum tūymaiyumāṉa vilaiyuyarnta melliya āṭai aṇinta
   viṇṇakappaṭaikaḷ veṇ kutiraikaḷil avaraippiṉ toṭarntaṉa.
   tive 19:15 nāṭukaḷait tākkuvataṟkāka avaruṭaiya vāyiliruntu kūrmaiyāṉa
   vāḷ oṉṟu vauiyē vantatu. avar iruppukkōl koṇṭu avarkaḷai naṭattuvār.
   ellām valla kaṭavuḷiṉ kaṭum cīṟṟam eṉṉum piḻivukkuḻiyil tirāṭcai
   iracattai avar piḻinteṭuppār.
   tive 19:16 'aracarkkellām aracar, āṇṭavarkkellām āṇṭavar' eṉṟa peyar
   avaruṭaiya āṭaiyilum toṭaiyilum eḻutappaṭṭiruntatu.
   tive 19:17 piṉṉar oru vāṉatūtar katiravaṉmītu niṟpatai nāṉ kaṇṭēṉ. avar
   naṭuvāṉil paṟantukoṇṭirunta ellāp paṟavaikaḷaiyum pārttu, uratta
   kuralil katti, "vāruṅkaḷ, kaṭavuḷ aḷikkum perum viruntukku vantu
   kūṭuṅkaḷ.
   tive 19:18 aracarkaḷ, āyirattavar talaivarkaḷ, valiyōr, paṭaivīrarkaḷ,
   kutiraikaḷ, kutirai vīrarkaḷ, urimaikkuṭimakkaḷ, aṭimaikaḷ, ciṟiyōr,
   periyōr ākiya aṉaivaruṭaiya cataiyaiyum tiṉṉa vāruṅkaḷ" eṉṟār.
   tive 19:19 anta vilaṅkum maṇṇulaka aracarkaḷum avarkaḷuṭaiya paṭaikaḷum
   kutiraimītu amairntiruntavarōṭum avaruṭaiya paṭaikaḷōṭum pōr
   toṭukkumāṟu kūṭiyirukkak kaṇṭēṉ.
   tive 19:20 avvilaṅku piṭipaṭṭatu. ataṉ muṉṉilaiyil arum aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷ
   ceytirunta pōli iṟaivākkiṉaṉum ataṉōṭu cērntu piṭipaṭṭāṉ. tāṉ ceyta
   arum aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷāl anta vilaṅkukkuriya kuṟi iṭṭukkoṇṭavarkaḷaiyum ataṉ
   cilaiyai vaṇaṅki vantavarkaḷaiyum ēmāṟṟiyavaṉ avaṉē. antap pōli
   iṟaivākkiṉaṉum vilaṅkum kantakam erintukoṇṭirunta neruppu ēriyil
   uyirōṭu eṟiyappaṭṭārkaḷ.
   tive 19:21 maṟṟavarkaḷ kutiraimītu amarntiruntavaruṭaiya vāyiṉiṉṟu
   vauiyē vanta vāḷāl kollappaṭṭārkaḷ. paṟavaikaḷellām avarkaḷiṉ cataiyai
   vayiṟārat tiṉṟaṉa.
   -----------
   20-m atikāram
   tive 20:1 piṉṉar vāṉatūtar oruvar viṇṇakattiliruntu iṟaṅkivarak kaṇṭēṉ.
   paṭukuḻiyiṉ tiṟavukōlum muraṭṭuc caṅkiliyum avar kaiyil iruntaṉa.
   tive 20:2 alakai eṉṟum cāttāṉ eṉṟum aḻaikkappaṭṭa arakkap pāmpai avar
   piṭittār. atuvē toṭakkattil irunta pāmpu. vāṉatūtar āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku
   ataik kaṭṭivaittār.
   tive 20:3 piṉṉar ataip paṭukuḻiyil taḷḷi, kuḻiyai aṭaittu,
   muttiraiyiṭṭār. ivvāṟu anta āyiram āṇṭukaḷ muṭiyumvarai nāṭukaḷai atu
   ēmāṟṟatavāṟu ceytār. itaṉpiṉ ciṟitu kālattukku atu kaṭṭaviḻttu
   viṭappaṭa vēṇṭum.
   tive 20:4 piṉpu nāṉ ariyaṇaikaḷaik kaṇṭēṉ. tīrppaḷikkum atikāram
   peṟṟirunta cilar avaṟṟiṉmītu vīṟṟiruntaṉar. kaṭavuḷiṉ vākkai aṟivittu
   iyēcuvukkuc cāṉṟu pakarntataṟkākat talai koṭuttavarkaḷiṉ āṉmākkaḷaiyum
   kaṇṭēṉ. avarkaḷ anta vilaṅkaiyō ataṉ cilaiyaiyō vaṇaṅkiyatillai.
   ataṟkuriya kuṟiyait taṅkaḷ neṟṟiyilō kaiyilō iṭṭukkoṇṭatumillai.
   avarkaḷ mīṇṭum uyirpeṟṟu, āyiram āṇṭukaḷ kiṟistuvōṭu āṭci purintārkaḷ.
   tive 20:5 iṟanta ēṉaiyōr anta āyiram āṇṭukaḷ muṭiyumvarai uyir
   peṟavillai. ituvē mutalām uyitteḻutal.
   tive 20:6 inta mutalām uyitteḻutalil paṅku peṟuvōr pēṟupeṟṟōr. avarkaḷ
   tūyōr āvar. avarkaḷmītu iraṇṭām cāvukku atikāram illai. avarkaḷ
   kaṭavuḷukkum kiṟistuvukkum paṇipuriyum kurukkaḷāy iruppārkaḷ.
   kiṟistuvōṭu āyiram āṇṭukaḷ āṭcipurivārkaḷ.
   tive 20:7 anta āyiram āṇṭukaḷ muṭintatum cāttāṉ ciṟaiyiliruntu
   kaṭṭaviḻttu viṭappaṭuvāṉ.
   tive 20:8 maṇṇakattiṉ nāṉku tikkukaḷilum uḷḷa nāṭukaḷai, atāvatu kōku,
   mākōku eṉpavaṟṟai ēmāṟṟavum, aṅkiruntu kaṭal maṇal pōṉṟa
   peruntokaiyiṉaraip pōrukku oṉṟu tiraṭṭavum avaṉ puṟappaṭṭuc celvāṉ.
   tive 20:9 avarkaḷ maṇṇulakameṅkum paravic ceṉṟu, iṟaimakkaḷiṉ
   pācaṟaiyaiyum kaṭavuḷiṉ aṉpukkuriya nakaraiyum cūḻntu koṇṭārkaḷ. āṉāl,
   neruppu vāṉattiliruntu vantu avarkaḷaic cuṭṭerittatu.
   tive 20:10 piṉpu avarkaḷai ēmāṟṟi vanta alakai kantaka, neruppu ēriyil
   eṟiyappaṭṭatu. aṅkētāṉ anta vilaṅkum ataṉ pōli iṟaivākkiṉaṉum
   eṟiyappaṭṭiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ iravu pakalāka eṉṟeṉṟum
   vataikkappaṭuvārkaḷ.
   tive 20:11 piṉpu periya, veṇmaiyāṉa ōr ariyaṇaiyaik kaṇṭēṉ. atil oruvar
   vīṟṟiruntār. avar muṉṉilaiyil maṇṇakamum viṇṇakamum irunta iṭam
   teriyāmal maṟaintuviṭṭaṉa.
   tive 20:12 iṟantōruḷ ciṟiyōr, periyōr ākiya aṉaivarum anta ariyaṇaimuṉ
   niṟkak kaṇṭēṉ. appoḻutu nūlkaḷ tiṟantu vaikkappaṭṭaṉa. vēṟoru nūlum
   tiṟantu vaikkappaṭṭatu, atu vāḻviṉ nūl. iṟantōriṉ ceyalkaḷ annūlkaḷil
   eḻutappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avaṟṟukku ēṟpa avarkaḷukkut tīrppu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu.
   tive 20:13 piṉṉar kaṭal taṉṉakattē irunta iṟantōrai vauiyēṟṟiyatu.
   atupōlac cāvum, pātāḷamum tammakattē irunta iṟantōrai vauiyēṟṟiṉa.
   avarkaḷ ovvoruvarukkum avaravar ceyalkaḷukku ēṟpat tīrppu
   vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu.
   tive 20:14 cāvum pātāḷamum neruppu ēriyil eṟiyappaṭṭaṉa. inta neruppu
   ēriyē iraṇṭām cāvu.
   tive 20:15 vāḻviṉ nūlil peyar eḻutappaṭātōr neruppu ēriyil
   eṟiyappaṭṭārkaḷ.
   ---------
   21-m atikāram
   tive 21:1 piṉpu nāṉ putiyatoru viṇṇakattaiyum putiyatoru maṇṇakattaiyum
   kaṇṭēṉ. muṉpu irunta viṇṇakamum maṇṇakamum maṟaintuviṭṭaṉa. kaṭalum
   illāmaṟ pōyiṟṟu.
   tive 21:2 appoḻutu putiya erucalēm eṉṉum tirunakar kaṭavuḷiṭamiruntu
   viṇṇakattai viṭṭu iṟaṅkivarak kaṇṭēṉ. taṉ maṇamakaṉukkākat taṉṉaiyē aṇi
   ceytu koṇṭa maṉamakaḷaippōl atu āyattamāy iruntatu.
   tive 21:3 piṉpu viṇṇakattiliruntu eḻunta perum kural oṉṟaik kēṭṭēṉ.
   atu, "itō! kaṭavuḷiṉ uṟaiviṭam maṉitar naṭuvē uḷḷatu. avar avarkaḷ
   naṭuvē kuṭiyiruppār. avarkaḷ avarukku makkaḷāy iruppārkaḷ. kaṭavuḷtāmē
   avarkaḷōṭu iruppār. avarē avarkaḷuṭaiya kaṭavuḷāy iruppār.
   tive 21:4 avarkaḷuṭaiya kaṇṇīr aṉaittaiyum avar tuṭaittu viṭuvār.
   iṉimēl cāvu irātu, tuyaram irātu. muṉpu iruntavaiyellām
   maṟaintuviṭṭaṉa" eṉṟatu.
   tive 21:5 appoḻutu ariyaṇaiyil vīṟṟiruntavar, "itō! nāṉ aṉaittaiyum
   putiyatu ākkukiṟēṉ" eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. mēlum, "'ivvākkukaḷ nampattakkavai,
   uṇmaiyuḷḷavai' eṉa eḻutu" eṉṟār.
   tive 21:6 piṉṉar avar eṉṉiṭam kūṟiyatu: "ellām niṟaivēṟiviṭṭatu.
   akaramum ṉakaramum nāṉē. tākamāy iruppōrukku vāḻvu aḷikkum
   nīrūṟṟiliruntu nāṉ ilavacamāyk kuṭikkak koṭuppēṉ.
   tive 21:7 veṟṟi peṟuvōr ivaṟṟai urimaippēṟāka peṟuvar. nāṉ avarkaḷukkuk
   kaṭavuḷāy iruppēṉ. avarkaḷ eṉakku makkaḷāy iruppārkaḷ.
   tive 21:8 āṉāl kōḻaikaḷ, nampikkai illātōr, aruvaruppukkuriyōr,
   kolaiyāḷikaḷ, parattaimaiyil īṭupaṭuvōr, cūṉiyakkārar,
   cilaivaḻipāṭṭiṉar, poyyar ākiya aṉaivarukkum, neruppu kantakamum eriyum
   ēriyē uriya paṅku ākum. ituvē iraṇṭām cāvu."
   tive 21:9 iṟutiyāṉa anta ēḻu vātaikaḷ niṟainta ēḻu kiṇṇaṅkaḷaik
   koṇṭirunta ēḻu vāṉatūtarkaḷuḷ oruvar vantu, "vā, āṭṭukkuṭṭi
   maṇantukoṇṭa maṇamakaḷai uṉakkuk kāṭṭuvēṉ" eṉṟu eṉṉiṭam kūṟiṉār.
   tive 21:10 tūya āvi eṉṉai āṭkoḷḷavē, anta vāṉatūtar oru periya, uyarnta
   malaikku eṉṉaik koṇṭuceṉṟār. tirunakarāṉa erucalēm kaṭavuḷiṭamiruntu
   viṇṇakattai viṭṭu iṟaṅki varuvatai eṉakkuk kāṭṭiṉār.
   tive 21:11 atil kaṭavuḷiṉ māṭci viḷaṅkiṟṟu. vilaiyuyarnta kalpōṉṟum
   paṭikakkalpōṉṟum ataṉ oḷi paḷiṅkeṉat tulaṅkiyatu.
   tive 21:12 ataiccuṟṟi periya, uyarnta matilum atil paṉṉiraṇṭu
   vāyilkaḷum iruntaṉa. vāyilkaḷukkup poṟuppāyp paṉṉiraṇṭu vāṉatūtarkaḷ
   niṉṟārkaḷ. isrēyal makkaḷuṭaiya paṉṉiraṇṭu kulaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum
   avvāyilkaḷil poṟikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
   tive 21:13 kiḻakkē mūṉṟum vaṭakkē mūṉṟum teṟkē mūṉṟum mēṟkē mūṉṟumāka
   avai amaintiruntaṉa.
   tive 21:14 nakariṉ matil paṉṉiraṇṭu aṭikkaṟḷaik koṇṭiruntatu. avaṟṟil
   āṭṭukkuṭṭiyiṉ paṉṉiraṇṭu tiruttūtarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum eḻutappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
   tive 21:15 eṉṉōṭu pēciyavar nakaraiyum ataṉ matilaiyum vāyilkaḷaiyum
   aḷakkumporuṭṭup poṉṉālāṉa ōr aḷavukōlai vaittiruntār.
   tive 21:16 annakaram caturamāy iruntatu. ataṉ nīḷamum akalamum orē
   aḷavutāṉ. avar anta aḷavukōḷaik koṇṭu nakarai aḷantār. ataṉ aḷavu
   iraṇṭāyirattu nāṉūṟu kilō mīṭṭar. ataṉ nīḷam, akalam, uyaram ellāmē orē
   aḷavutāṉ.
   tive 21:17 piṉ avar matilai aḷantār. ataṉ uyaram irunūṟṟup patiṉāṟu
   aṭi. maṉitariṭaiyē vaḻakkil irunta aḷavaikaḷaiyē avarum
   payaṉpaṭuttiṉār.
   tive 21:18 ataṉ matil paṭikak kaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntatu. annakarō
   tūymaiyāṉa, kaṇṇāṭi pōṉṟa pacumpoṉṉāl āṉatu.
   tive 21:19 nakara matiliṉ aṭikkaṟkaḷ vilaiyuyarnta ellāvita kaṟkaḷālum
   aṇiceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa. mutalāvatu paṭikak kal, iraṇṭāvatu nīlamaṇi,
   mūṉṟāvatu palavaṇṇappaṭikam, nāṉkāvatu marakatam.
   tive 21:20 aintāvatu kōmētakam, āṟāvatu māṇikkam, ēḻāvatu poṟkal,
   eṭṭāvatu paṭikappaccai, oṉpatāvatu puṭparākam, pattāvatu vaiṭūriyam,
   patiṉoṉṟāvatu patumarākam, paṉṉiraṇṭāvatu cukanti.
   tive 21:21 paṉṉiraṇṭu vāyilkaḷum paṉṉiraṇṭu muttukaḷāl āṉavai. ovvoru
   vāyilum ovvoru vakaiyāṉa muttāl āṉatu. nakariṉ teru teḷḷat tauivāṉa
   kaṇṇāṭipōṉṟa pacumpoṉṉāl āṉatu.
   tive 21:22 nakarukkuḷ kōvil kāṇappaṭavillai. ēṉeṉil ellām valla
   kaṭavuḷākiya āṇṭavarum āṭṭukkuṭṭiyumē ataṉ kōvil.
   tive 21:23 annakarukku oḷi koṭukkak katiravaṉō nilavō tēvaippaṭavillai.
   kaṭavuḷiṉ māṭciyē ataṉ oḷi. āṭṭukkuṭṭiyē ataṉ viḷakku.
   tive 21:24 makkaḷiṉattār ataṉ oḷiyil naṭappar. maṇṇulaka aracarkaḷ
   taṅkaḷukkup perumai cērppavaṟṟaiyellām aṅkuk koṇṭu celvārkaḷ.
   tive 21:25 ataṉ vāyilkaḷ nāḷ muḻuvatum tiṟantirukkum. aṅku iravē irātu.
   tive 21:26 ulakiṉ perumaiyum māṇpum ellām aṅkuk koṇṭu cellappaṭum.
   tive 21:27 āṉāl tīṭṭuppaṭṭatu etuvum orupōtu atil nuḻaiyātu. atupōl
   aruvaruppāṉataic ceyvōrum poyyarum atil nuḻaiyamāṭṭārkaḷ. āṭṭukkuṭṭi
   vaittirukkum vāḻviṉ nūlil peyar eḻutappaṭṭavarkaḷ maṭṭumē atil
   nuḻaivārkaḷ.
   ----------
   22-m atikāram
   tive 22:1 piṉpu anta vāṉatūtar vāḻvu aḷikkum taṇṇīr ōṭikkoṇṭirunta ōr
   āṟṟai eṉakkuk kāṭṭiṉār. atu paḷiṅkupōl oḷirntatu. atu kaṭavuḷum
   āṭṭukkuṭṭiyum vīṟṟirunta ariyaṇaiyiliruntu puṟappaṭṭu,
   tive 22:2 nakariṉ teru naṭuvē pāyntōṭiyatu. āṟṟiṉ iru maruṅkum
   vāḻvutarum maram iruntatu. mātattiṟku orumuṟaiyāka atu āṇṭutōṟum
   paṉṉiru muṟai kaṉikaḷ tarum. ataṉ ilaikaḷ makkaḷiṉaṅkaḷaik
   kuṇappaṭuttak kūṭiyavai.
   tive 22:3 cāpattukku uḷḷāṉatu etuvum nakaril irātu. kaṭavuḷum
   āṭṭukkuṭṭiyum vīṟṟirukkum ariyaṇai aṅku irukkum. kaṭavuḷiṉ paṇiyāḷarkaḷ
   avarai vaḻipaṭuvārkaḷ.
   tive 22:4 avaratu mukattaik kāṇpārkaḷ. avaratu peyar avarkaḷuṭaiya
   neṟṟiyil eḻutappaṭṭirukkum.
   tive 22:5 iṉi iravē irātu. viḷakkiṉ oḷiyō katiravaṉiṉ oḷiyō
   avarkaḷukkut tēvaippaṭātu. ēṉeṉil kaṭavuḷākiya āṇṭavar avarkaḷmītu oḷi
   vīcuvār. avarkaḷ eṉṟeṉṟum āṭcipurivārkaḷ.
   tive 22:6 piṉṉar anta vāṉatūtar eṉṉiṭam, "ivvākkukaḷ nampattakkavai,
   uṇmaiyuḷḷavai. viraivil nikaḻavēṇṭiyavaṟṟait tam paṇiyāḷarkaḷukkuk
   kāṭṭumāṟu, iṟaivākkiṉarait tūṇṭiyeḻuppum kaṭavuḷākiya āṇṭavar tam
   vāṉatūtarai aṉuppiṉār.
   tive 22:7 itō! nāṉ viraivil varukiṟēṉ" eṉṟār. inta nūlil uḷḷa
   iṟaivākkukaḷaik kaṭaippiṭippōr pēṟupeṟṟōr.
   tive 22:8 yōvāṉākiya nāṉē ivaṟṟaiyellām kaṇṭēṉ, kēṭṭēṉ. appoḻutu
   ivaṟṟai eṉakkuk kāṭṭiya vāṉatūtarai vaṇaṅkum poruṭṭu avaruṭaiya
   kālaṭiyil viḻuntēṉ.
   tive 22:9 avarō eṉṉiṭam, "vēṇṭām. uṉakkum iṟaivākkiṉarkaḷāṉa uṉ
   cakōtarar cakōtarikaḷukkum, inta nūlil uḷḷa vākkukaḷaik
   kaṭaipiṭippōrukkum nāṉ uṭaṉ paṇiyāḷaṉē. kaṭavuḷai maṭṭumē nī vaṇaṅka
   vēṇṭum" eṉṟār.
   tive 22:10 avar toṭarntu eṉṉiṭam piṉvarumāṟu kūṟiṉār: "inta nūlil uḷḷa
   iṟaivākkukaḷai muttiraiyiṭṭu vaikkātē. itō! kālam neruṅki vantuviṭṭatu.
   tive 22:11 itaṟkiṭaiyil, tīṅkupurivōr tīṅkupurintukoṇṭē irukkaṭṭum.
   iḻukkāṉavaṟṟaic ceyvōr iḻukkāṉavaṟṟaic ceytu koṇṭē irukkaṭṭum. tūyōr
   tūymaiyāṉavaṟṟaic ceytukoṇṭē irukkaṭṭum.
   tive 22:12 "itō! nāṉ viraivil varukiṟēṉ. avaravar ceyalukku ēṟpa
   avaravarukku nāṉ aḷikkavirukkiṉṟa kaimmāṟu eṉṉiṭam uḷḷatu.
   tive 22:13 akaramum ṉakaramum, mutalum iṟutiyum, toṭakkamum muṭivum
   nāṉē.
   tive 22:14 "taṅkaḷ āṭaikaḷait tuvaittuk koṇṭōr pēṟupeṟṟōr. vāḻvu tarum
   marattiṉmītu avarkaḷukku urimai uṇṭu. avarkaḷ vāyilkal vaḻiyāka
   nakarukkuḷ nuḻaivārkaḷ.
   tive 22:15 naṭattaikeṭṭōr, cūṉiyakkārar, parattaimaiyil īṭupaṭuvōr,
   kolaiyāḷikaḷ, cilaivaḻipāṭṭiṉar, poymmai nāṭi ataṉpaṭi naṭappōr ākiya
   aṉaivarum atil nuḻaiyamāṭṭārkaḷ.
   tive 22:16 "tiruccapaikaḷukkāka uṅkaḷ muṉ ivvāṟu cāṉṟu pakarumāṟu
   iyēcuvākiya nāṉ eṉ vāṉatūtarai aṉuppiyuḷḷēṉ. tāvītiṉ kulakkoḻuntum,
   vaḻittōṉṟalum nāṉē! oḷipaṭaitta viṭiveḷḷiyum nāṉē!
   tive 22:17 tūya āviyārum āṭṭukkuṭṭiyiṉ maṇamakaḷum cērntu,
   "varuka!varuka!" eṉkiṟārkaḷ. itaikkēṭpōrum, "varuka!varuka!" eṉac
   collaṭṭum. tākamāy iruppōr eṉṉiṭam varaṭṭum. viruppam uḷḷōr vāḻvu tarum
   taṇṇīrai ilavacamāyk kuṭikkaṭṭum."
   tive 22:18 inta nūlil uḷḷa iṟaivākkukaḷaik kēṭpōr aṉaivaraiyum
   yōvāṉākiya nāṉ eccarippatu yāteṉil: inta iṟaivākkukaḷōṭu etaiyāvatu
   yārēṉum cērttāl, innūlil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷa vātaikaḷ avarkaḷai
   vantaṭaiyumāṟu kaṭavuḷ ceyvār.
   tive 22:19 inta nūlil uḷḷa iṟaivākkukaḷuḷ etaiyāvatu yārēṉum eṭuttu
   viṭṭāl, inta nūlil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷa vāḻvu tarum marattiṉiṉṟum
   tirunakariṉiṉṟum avarkaḷukkuriya paṅkaik kaṭavuḷ eṭuttuviṭuvār.
   tive 22:20 ivaṟṟukkuc cāṉṟu pakarpavar, "ām, viraivākavē varukiṟēṉ"
   eṉkiṟār. āmeṉ. āṇṭavarākiya iyēcuvē, vārum.
   tive 22:21 āṇṭavarākiya iyēcuviṉ aruḷ aṉaivarōṭum iruppatāka!
   ----------

                      putiya ēṟpāṭu muṭintatu!!
